中文摘要：拒绝采样微调（RFT）被广泛用于训练代码智能体，方法是在可验证的软件工程任务上生成轨迹，保留那些通过测试的轨迹，并在成功的样本上进行微调。然而，即使是强大的代码智能体也会在相当一部分此类任务上反复失败，而标准RFT只是丢弃这些失败样本。被丢弃的样本恰恰是最困难且信息量最大的样本，它们来自代价高昂且可验证的实例。更强的基座模型可能会减少失败数量，但剩余的困难案例仍然定义了进一步改进的前沿。我们提出FailForge，一个将失败轨迹转化为训练信号的智能体框架。对于每个失败实例，智能体根据错误反馈和执行轨迹诊断失败原因，将诊断提炼为简洁且可操作的技能，并将该技能注入智能体上下文以进行有指导的第二次尝试。在技能指导下成功的轨迹会被重新纳入RFT语料库。关键在于，技能在训练时被移除，因此模型内化恢复出的行为，而不是在推理时依赖外部提示。FailForge以极小的额外成本恢复了超过26%先前失败的实例，并在增强语料库上训练Qwen3.5-4B，使SWE-bench Verified解决率比强RFT基线提高6.6个百分点，且增益集中在最困难的问题上。

Original abstract: Rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) is widely used to train code agents by generating trajectories on verifiable software engineering tasks, retaining those that pass the tests, and fine-tuning on the successful rollouts. However, even strong code agents repeatedly fail on a substantial fraction of such tasks, and standard RFT simply discards these failures. The discarded samples are precisely the hardest and most informative ones, drawn from verifiable instances that are costly to curate. Stronger base models may reduce the number of failures, but the remaining hard cases still define the frontier for further improvement. We propose FailForge, an agentic framework that converts failed rollouts into training signal. For each failed instance, an agent diagnoses the failure from error feedback and execution traces, distills the diagnosis into a concise and actionable skill, and injects the skill into the agent context for a guided second attempt. Trajectories that succeed under skill guidance are folded back into the RFT corpus. Crucially, the skill is removed at training time, so the model internalizes the recovered behavior rather than relying on external hints at inference. FailForge recovers over 26% of previously failed instances at marginal additional cost, and training Qwen3.5-4B on the augmented corpus improves the SWE-bench Verified resolve rate by 6.6 points over a strong RFT baseline, with gains concentrated on the hardest problems.

</details>


### [57] [Unaccountable Delegation, Fading Skills: Mapping the Risks of Workplace AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08601)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Lakmal Meegahapola,Edyta Bogucka,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Elizabeth Black,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 分析工作场所AI代理风险与技能退化，对agent安全与人类协作设计有参考意义。

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向AI代理的工作场所风险分类体系，通过分析2,078项任务生成8,356个风险场景，提出15类风险，并发现增强模式存在技能侵蚀风险，且风险源于人机协作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险分类体系过于宽泛，无法涵盖AI代理在具体工作岗位中引入的特殊风险，组织需要针对性的分类体系以预测和缓解这些风险。

Method: 通过文献综述构建包含代理、目标和环境的多层框架，嵌入结构化提示应用于O*NET任务描述，生成风险场景并验证（45名工人+LLM评判），最后扩展为15类风险分类体系。

Result: 识别出4个关键发现：增强并非安全；错误的代理行为风险最高且集中于人机边界；自动化与组织风险相关，增强与工人风险相关；工人偏好本分类体系（64%偏好）。

Conclusion: 工作场所AI代理风险取决于人机协作方式与部署模式，更安全的工作场所需要同时改进代理本身和设计人机协作机制。

中文摘要：为了预测AI代理带来的社会技术风险，组织需要对其进行分类的体系。然而，现有的AI风险分类体系侧重于广义风险，并未涵盖代理引入的特定工作风险。为弥补这一空白，我们做出了三项主要贡献。首先，我们基于对AI代理的文献回顾开发了一个多层框架。该框架建模了三个核心组件及其交互：代理、目标和环境。其次，我们将该框架嵌入到结构化提示中，并将其应用于O*NET数据库中2,078个工作任务描述，生成了8,356个按严重性和部署模式（自动化或增强）标记的风险场景。我们与10个工作岗位的45名工人以及一个独立的LLM评判员对这些场景进行了验证，确认了其合理性和与工作任务的一致性。最后，我们扩展了一个现有分类体系，创建了一个涵盖所有风险场景的15类别工作场所AI代理风险分类。我们的分析突出了四个发现。第一，增强并非天生安全，因为对代理的过度依赖会逐渐侵蚀工人的技能和监督能力。第二，错误的代理行为占风险场景的最大份额，并且严重风险集中度最高。许多风险出现在人机边界。第三，自动化主要与组织风险相关，而增强主要与工人风险相关。第四，工人发现我们的分类体系在风险分类任务中比其他两个分类体系更易于使用，并且在与近期生成式AI风险分类的非平局比较中，有64%的偏好选择。这些发现表明，工作场所AI代理风险并非仅由代理本身产生；它们还取决于人们如何与代理协作以及代理如何部署。更安全的工作场所不仅需要更安全的代理，还需要精心设计的人机协作。

Original abstract: To anticipate socio-technical risks from AI agents, organizations need taxonomies to classify them. However, existing AI risk taxonomies focus on broad risks and do not capture job-specific risks introduced by agents. To address this gap, we make three main contributions. First, we developed a multi-layer framework from a literature review of AI agents. The framework models three core components and their interactions: agents, goals, and environment. Second, we embedded this framework in a structured prompt and applied it to descriptions of 2,078 job tasks from the O*NET database, producing 8,356 risk scenarios labeled by severity and deployment mode (automation or augmentation). We validated these scenarios with 45 workers across 10 job roles and an independent LLM judge, confirming their plausibility and alignment with job tasks. Finally, we extended an existing taxonomy to create a 15-category taxonomy of workplace AI agent risks that covers all our risk scenarios. Our analysis highlights four findings. First, augmentation is not inherently safe because overreliance on agents can gradually erode workers' skills and oversight. Second, Erroneous Agent Actions accounts for the largest share of risk scenarios and has the highest concentration of severe risks. Many arise at the human-agent boundary. Third, automation is associated mainly with organizational risks, while augmentation is associated mainly with risks to workers. Fourth, workers found our taxonomy easier to use for a risk classification task than two other taxonomies and preferred it in 64% of non-tied comparisons with a recent generative AI risk taxonomy. These findings show that workplace AI agent risks do not arise from agents alone; they also depend on how people work with agents and how agents are deployed. Safer workplaces require not only safer agents but also carefully designed human-AI agent collaboration.

</details>


### [58] [ForestBench: A Unified Graph Framework for Evaluating Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08605)
*Guo Chen,Ziwen Li,Reed Li,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Bingbing Xu,Junjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出统一图框架评估多agent协作，可迁移到coding agent的benchmark设计。

TL;DR: 提出ForestBench，一个基于统一协作图和参考森林的框架，用于快速、无需额外LLM推理地评估多智能体系统的协作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估方法缺乏统一基础：结果基准忽略协作，LLM评审需要额外推理且不稳定，因此需要一种可泛化、结构化的评估方式。

Method: 将MAS原生轨迹映射为统一协作图，与基准预构建的查询特定参考森林（成功图集合）比较，并使用多种指标评分；实例化为ForestBench，包含844个协作必需查询和每查询10个参考图。

Result: 在六个代表性MAS框架上验证了框架的有效性，并通过受控研究测试了稳定性；评分在毫秒级完成，无需额外LLM推理。

Conclusion: ForestBench为比较多样化MAS协作轨迹提供了可复用、高效且稳定的结构基础，有望成为标准评估工具。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）正在迅速 proliferating，但其异构执行轨迹无法为跨方法评估提供共同基础。仅基于结果的基准丢弃了协作信息，而LLM即评审（LLM-as-Judge）评估需要额外的、依赖模型的推理，并且可能随LLM和评分标准而变化。我们引入了一个可泛化的评估框架，将原生MAS轨迹映射到统一协作图的共享空间中，使不同方法能够在相同的表示、参考集和指标面板下进行评估。候选图与查询特定的参考森林进行比较。每个森林是基准提供的已验证成功图的集合：它记录了代表性MAS方法完成任务的各种方式，而不是规定唯一的最优过程。将该框架实例化为ForestBench，我们从七个公共数据集中筛选出844个需要协作的查询，为每个查询预计算十个成功的基于目标条件的参考图，并评估六个代表性的MAS框架。受控的骨干、参考构建和扰动研究测试了评估的稳定性和范围。一旦基准森林构建完成，ForestBench可在毫秒内对轨迹进行评分，无需进一步的LLM推理，为比较多样化MAS协作轨迹提供了可重复使用的结构基础。

Original abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) are proliferating rapidly, but their heterogeneous execution traces provide no common basis for evaluation across methods. Outcome-only benchmarks discard collaborations, whereas LLM-as-Judge evaluation requires additional, model-dependent inference and can vary with the LLM and rubric. We introduce a generalizable evaluation framework that maps native MAS traces into a shared space of unified collaboration graphs, enabling different methods to be evaluated under the same representation, reference set, and metric panel. Candidate graphs are compared with a query-specific reference forest. Each forest is a benchmark-provided collection of verified-success graphs: it records diverse ways in which representative MAS methods can complete the task, rather than prescribing a unique optimal process. Instantiating the framework as ForestBench, we filter $844$ collaboration-necessary queries from seven public datasets, precompute ten successful target-conditioned reference graphs per query, and evaluate six representative MAS frameworks. Controlled backbone, reference-construction, and perturbation studies test the stability and scope of evaluation. Once the benchmark forests are built, ForestBench scores a trace in milliseconds without further LLM inference, providing a reusable structural basis for comparing diverse MAS collaboration traces.

</details>


### [59] [MedCalc-R1: Knowledge-Guided Reward Framework for Medical Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08623)
*Haotian Wang,Lian Yan,Xingzhi Yao,Fanshu Meng,Ye He,Jingchi Jiang,Yi Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 11 figures, work in prograss

Selection reason: 提出知识引导的混合奖励框架用于RLVR数学推理，奖励设计与外部验证机制对agent训练有潜在迁移价值

TL;DR: 提出MedCalc-R1，一种用于数学推理RLVR的知识引导混合奖励框架，通过知识验证奖励和软硬混合奖励解决浮点结果评估中的阈值校准和精度问题，在安全关键领域表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于容差的奖励在RLVR数学推理任务中存在阈值校准困难、训练不稳定和精度不足，尤其在临床环境中更为突出。

Method: 提出MedCalc-R1，包括知识验证奖励机制（强制生成公式并外部验证）和混合软硬奖励方案（临床安全阈值硬约束与精度敏感软奖励结合）。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在推理准确性和泛化能力上显著优于现有基线，验证了其在安全关键领域的有效性。

Conclusion: MedCalc-R1通过知识引导和混合奖励设计，解决了浮点评估挑战，在安全关键应用中具有良好适用性。

中文摘要：在面向数学推理任务的基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）框架中，浮点结果通常使用基于容差的奖励进行评估。然而，这种策略存在阈值校准困难、训练动态不稳定以及精度有限等挑战，尤其是在临床场景中。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种知识引导的混合奖励框架（MedCalc-R1）。具体而言，我们引入了一种知识验证奖励机制，强制显式生成计算公式，并通过外部验证器进一步验证这些公式，以增强可解释性和推理可靠性。此外，我们设计了一种混合软硬奖励方案，将基于临床安全阈值的硬约束与对精度敏感的软奖励相结合，后者在可接受范围内逐步引导学习。实验结果表明，我们的方法在推理准确性和泛化能力上均显著优于现有基线，验证了其在安全关键领域中的有效性和适用性。

Original abstract: In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) frameworks for mathematical reasoning tasks, floating-point results are typically evaluated using a tolerance-based reward. However, this strategy suffers from challenges such as difficulty in threshold calibration, unstable training dynamics, and limited accuracy, especially in clinical scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge-guided hybrid reward framework (\textsc{MedCalc-R1}). Specifically, we introduce a knowledge verification reward mechanism that enforces explicit generation of computational formulas, which are further validated by an external verifier to enhance interpretability and reasoning reliability. Furthermore, we design a hybrid soft-hard reward scheme combining a hard constraint based on clinical safety thresholds with a soft, precision-sensitive reward that progressively guides learning within the acceptable range. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines in both reasoning accuracy and generalization capability, validating the effectiveness and applicability in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [60] [SkillReason: Reasoning-Enhanced Agent Skill Retrieval for Implicit User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08640)
*Donghong Jiang,Endian Lin,Luoping Cui,Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Fan Yang,Hong Wang,Zhao Yang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出推理增强的agent技能检索框架与benchmark，检索规划机制可迁移到coding agent技能库

TL;DR: 提出 SkillReason-Bench 基准和 SkillReason 两阶段框架，通过思维链推理作为训练监督，实现无需推理生成的高效技能检索，并在三个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能检索面对简洁且未充分指定的用户请求时效果不佳，且缺乏覆盖此类请求的大规模基准，导致高层任务目标与技能能力之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: SkillReason 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用更强教师生成的推理轨迹，通过对比学习、检索分布对齐和语言建模进行显式监督；第二阶段采用检索引导的 GRPO 目标优化推理轨迹，推理时直接编码查询，无需生成 CoT。

Result: 在 SkillReason-Bench、SkillRet 和 SRA-Bench 上均取得最先进性能，验证了推理增强训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 推理增强的训练方法能有效弥合高层任务目标与技能能力之间的语义差距，同时保持高效的仅查询检索。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体越来越依赖于可复用的技能来扩展其超越参数化知识的能力。然而，从大规模技能库中检索合适的技能仍然具有挑战性，因为现实中的用户请求往往简洁且不充分指定，仅陈述任务目标，而将所需能力和执行步骤隐式化。现有基准对此类请求的覆盖有限。为弥补这一空缺，我们引入了 SkillReason-Bench，一个大规模跨领域基准，包含 3,729 个查询和涵盖九个领域的 61,228 个技能的检索语料库。我们进一步提出了 SkillReason，一个两阶段框架，利用思维链推理作为技能检索的训练时监督。在第一阶段，由更强的教师生成的能力推理轨迹通过对比学习、检索分布对齐和语言建模提供显式监督，鼓励检索器在其查询表示中内化能力推理。在第二阶段，检索引导的 GRPO 目标鼓励模型探索更适合其自身能力且对检索更有效的推理轨迹。在推理时，SkillReason 直接编码原始查询，无需自回归 CoT 生成，保持了高效的仅查询检索。在 SkillReason-Bench、SkillRet 和 SRA-Bench 上的大量实验表明，SkillReason 在所有三个基准上均达到了最先进的性能，证明了推理增强训练更好地弥合了高层次任务目标与技能能力之间的语义鸿沟。

Original abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on reusable skills to extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowl- edge. However, retrieving the appropriate skill from a large- scale library remains challenging because realistic user re- quests are often concise and underspecified, stating only the task goal while leaving the required capabilities and execu- tion steps implicit. Existing benchmarks provide limited cov- erage of such requests. To address this gap, we introduce SkillReason-Bench, a large-scale cross-domain benchmark containing 3,729 queries and a retrieval corpus of 61,228 skills spanning nine domains. We further propose SkillRea- son, a two-stage framework that uses chain-of-thought rea- soning as training-time supervision for skill retrieval. In Stage I, capability reasoning traces generated by a stronger teacher provide explicit supervision through contrastive learning, re- trieval distribution alignment, and language modeling, en- couraging the retriever to internalize capability reasoning in its query representation. In Stage II, a retrieval-guided GRPO objective encourages the model to explore reasoning trajecto- ries better suited to its own capabilities and more effective for retrieval. At inference, SkillReason directly encodes the orig- inal query without autoregressive CoT generation, preserv- ing efficient query-only retrieval. Extensive experiments on SkillReason-Bench, SkillRet, and SRA-Bench show that Skill- Reason achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating that reasoning-enhanced training better bridges the semantic gap between high-level task goals and skill capabilities.

</details>


### [61] [The Scaffolding Matters More Than the Interface: A Controlled Comparison of MCP and CLI Tool Use Across Seven Agent Scaffoldings, Five Language Models, and One Software Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08654)
*Marc Alier Forment,María José Casañ Guerrero,Francisco José García-Peñalvo,Juanan Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 29 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables. Companion methodology paper: arXiv:2606.11869. Dataset and measurement harness (open source, GPL-3.0): https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21851992

Selection reason: 控制比较七种agent scaffoldings与MCP/CLI工具接口的开销，是coding agent harness设计的直接实证

TL;DR: 通过七个代理脚手架和五个语言模型的实验，发现MCP与CLI在工具使用成本上的差异不稳定，但脚手架对成本的影响巨大；CLI-only的脚手架比支持MCP的脚手架便宜5-28倍，且无MCP也能完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表估计分歧超过一个数量级，且基于不可复现的从业者报告；需要严格测量MCP与CLI的工具使用成本差异。

Method: 在七个代理脚手架和五个LM上运行同一软件任务（六次Git操作），检查仓库状态验证完成，对比CLI与MCP路径，并开源所有资源。

Result: 脚手架主导成本；两个无MCP的脚手架仅用CLI便宜5-28倍，270亿参数本地模型成本变化139倍；MCP/CLI比率不稳定（0.43x-29x），失败成本MCP更高（12.9% vs 2.2%），失败频率相当。

Conclusion: MCP对这类任务非必需，成本主要取决于脚手架；未验证实际行为的比较会测量未知混合，须开源验证。

中文摘要：AI编码代理的运行成本可能在更大程度上取决于驱动它的代理脚手架，而不是它访问工具所通过的接口。我们着手测量通过模型上下文协议（MCP）使用工具与通过普通命令行界面（CLI）使用工具的成本差异，而关于这一差异，已发表的估计相差超过一个数量级，且这些估计基于无法复现的从业者报告。我们在七个代理脚手架和五个语言模型上运行了一个固定的软件任务——对私有在线Git仓库进行六次操作——并通过检查仓库状态而非信任代理的自我报告来验证完成情况。主导效应是脚手架。七个中有两个完全不支持MCP；它们仅使用CLI就完成了每次运行，这表明MCP对于这类工作并非必需，并且与五个支持MCP的脚手架相比，仅比较CLI运行（且未在任何地方连接MCP服务器）时，它们便宜5.0倍到28倍。该效应对于一个本地运行的270亿参数小模型来说最大，其成本在不同脚手架上变化了139倍，而它在所有脚手架上均完成了任务。我们原本要进行的比较被证明是不稳定的：十三个严格配对的MCP-to-CLI比率跨越0.43倍到29倍，两边都有离群值。这两种接口在失败成本上有所区分，MCP运行中花费的12.9%的钱没有买到任何完成的工作，而CLI运行中这一比例为2.2%，但在失败频率上没有区别：在原始运行及其重复中，两者的失败率同样常见。代理经常忽略分配给它们的接口，因此不验证实际行为的比较衡量的是未知的混合。测试框架、任务、验证和完整数据集均以开源方式发布。

Original abstract: How much an AI coding agent costs to run can depend more on the agent scaffolding that drives it than on the interface through which it
  reaches its tools. We set out to measure the cost of tool use over the Model Context Protocol (MCP) against tool use over an ordinary
  command-line interface (CLI), a difference on which published estimates disagree by more than an order of magnitude while resting on
  practitioner reports that cannot be reproduced. We ran one fixed software task -- six operations against a private online git repository --
  across seven agent scaffoldings and five language models, and we verified completion by inspecting the repository state rather than trusting
  the agent's self-report. The dominant effect was the scaffolding. Two of the seven ship no MCP support at all; they completed every run using
  only the CLI, which shows that MCP is unnecessary for this class of work, and they were 5.0x to 28x cheaper than the five scaffoldings that
  do support MCP, comparing CLI runs alone with no MCP server attached anywhere. The effect was largest for a small 27-billion-parameter model
  running locally, whose cost varied 139x across scaffoldings while it completed the task under all of them. The comparison we set out to make
  proved unstable: thirteen strictly paired MCP-to-CLI ratios span 0.43x to 29x, with outliers on both sides. The two interfaces separate on
  the cost of failure, where 12.9 per cent of the money spent on MCP runs bought no completed work against 2.2 per cent on CLI runs, but not on
  its frequency: failures were equally common in both, in the original runs and in their repetitions alike. Agents frequently ignored the
  interface they were assigned, so comparisons that do not verify actual behaviour measure an unknown mixture. The harness, the task, the
  verification and the complete dataset are released as open source.

</details>


### [62] [Branch2Skill: Efficient Skill Evolution Through Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08677)
*Yanwei Ren,Haotian Zhang,Likang Xiao,Jiaxing Huang,Jiayan Qiu,Baosheng Yu,Quan Chen,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Technical report

Selection reason: 利用推理树生成密集监督进行技能演化，提升agent技能更新效率并显著降低token开销，可迁移到代码优化agent

TL;DR: Branch2Skill利用蒙特卡洛树搜索从单个推理树生成多样化轨迹，通过比较精英路径与兄弟路径提取逐步证据，并将其蒸馏为可复用更新，从而显著降低令牌成本并提升技能进化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能进化方法依赖单条轨迹，早期推理错误会传播并削弱反馈，导致需要重复展开-更新循环，产生高额令牌成本。

Method: Branch2Skill在固定预算下执行蒙特卡洛树搜索获得多条推理轨迹，将精英路径与共享前缀的兄弟路径进行对比，提取逐步证据，然后将多步证据蒸馏为可复用更新，使一个推理树提供密集监督。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，Branch2Skill一致提升任务性能并提高技能进化效率；使用GPT 5.5时，相比SkillOpt节省73.2%令牌且性能更优。

Conclusion: 推理树不仅能支持更有效的轨迹搜索，还能为技能改进提供更丰富的监督，实现更高效的技能进化。

中文摘要：技能进化通过随时间推移的反馈来改进智能体技能，而失败的轨迹通常通过揭示不完整或误导性行为来提供信息丰富的信号。然而，现有方法主要依赖单条轨迹，其中早期的推理错误可能传播到后续步骤，并削弱可用于技能细化的反馈。因此，改进技能需要重复的展开、诊断和更新循环，从而产生大量的令牌成本。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了Branch2Skill，一个高效的框架，将单个推理树转换为密集的监督信号用于技能进化。对于每个任务或问题，Branch2Skill在固定预算下执行蒙特卡洛树搜索以获得多样化的推理轨迹，然后将精英路径与共享相同前缀的兄弟替代方案进行比较，以提取关于哪些推理模式应保留、修改或避免的逐步证据。最后，Branch2Skill将多步证据提炼为可复用的更新，使得一个推理树能够为多个推理步骤提供监督，并减少重复展开-更新循环的需求。在涵盖推理和智能体任务的六个基准测试中，Branch2Skill一致地提升了任务性能，同时提高了技能进化效率。例如，以GPT 5.5作为目标模型，Branch2Skill相比SkillOpt使用了73.2%更少的令牌，同时实现了更优的性能。这些结果表明，推理树不仅能支持更有效的轨迹搜索，还能为更高效的技能改进提供更丰富的监督。代码将公开。

Original abstract: Skill evolution improves agent skills through feedback over time, with failed trajectories often providing informative signals by revealing incomplete or misleading behaviors. However, existing methods mainly rely on single trajectories, where early reasoning errors can propagate through subsequent steps and weaken the feedback available for skill refinement. Consequently, improving skills requires repeated cycles of rollout, diagnosis, and update, incurring substantial token costs. To address this challenge, we introduce Branch2Skill, an efficient framework that transforms a single reasoning tree into dense supervision for skill evolution. For each task or problem, Branch2Skill performs Monte Carlo tree search under a fixed budget to obtain diverse reasoning trajectories, then compares an elite path with sibling alternatives sharing the same prefixes to extract step-wise evidence about which reasoning patterns to retain, revise, or avoid. Finally, Branch2Skill distills multi-step evidence into reusable updates, allowing one reasoning tree to provide supervision across multiple reasoning steps and reducing the need for repeated rollout-update cycles. Across six benchmarks covering reasoning and agentic tasks, Branch2Skill consistently improves task performance while enhancing skill evolution efficiency. For example, with GPT 5.5 as the target model, Branch2Skill uses 73.2% fewer tokens than SkillOpt, while achieving superior performance. These results demonstrate that reasoning trees can support not only more effective trajectory search, but also richer supervision for more efficient skill improvement. Code will be published.

</details>


### [63] [EnergyBridge: Benchmarking Household Energy Management, User Participation, and Grid Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08691)
*Xudong Wu,Zeqing Wu,Jiarui Zhang,Xuhao Fan,Ziang Ding,Yuming Zhuang,Mingqi Yuan,Yilun Du,Hongjie Jia,Yunfei Mu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 能源管理agent基准包含LLM用户参与模拟器与授权机制，对agent评测有一般启发但垂直领域为主

TL;DR: 提出了EnergyBridge基准与智能体框架，连接容量报告、用户授权与物理执行，在天津和柏林场景中实现更高的模拟授权率、更低的事件窗口能耗和更可靠的容量承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估控制策略而忽略了事件特定的用户授权，导致物理灵活性无法转化为可靠的容量，因此需要一种将用户授权纳入评估的基准和框架。

Method: EnergyBridge结合天津和柏林区域特定的EnergyPlus物理环境与基于LLM的用户参与模拟器，连接容量报告、家庭授权和物理执行，并通过584个人类角色扮演判断进行验证。

Result: LLM用户参与模拟器以5.3点平均绝对接受误差保留方法排序；EnergyBridge在传统控制器和智能体基线中，于两个地区均取得最高模拟授权、最低事件窗口能耗和最可靠的容量承诺。

Conclusion: EnergyBridge提供了可复现的以人为中心的电网灵活性研究基准，并公开发布人类数据和代码，以促进该领域的发展。

中文摘要：住宅虚拟电厂（VPP）可以通过转移家庭需求来提供电网灵活性，但只有当居民授权计划并兑现承诺的响应时，物理灵活性才能成为可靠的容量。现有基准评估控制但忽略了特定事件的授权。我们提出了EnergyBridge，一个连接容量报告、家庭授权和物理执行的基准与智能体框架。它结合了天津和柏林特定区域的EnergyPlus环境与基于LLM的用户参与模拟器。针对584个与人物和事件匹配的人类角色扮演判断，基于LLM的用户参与模拟器以5.3点的平均绝对接受误差保留了方法排序。在传统控制器和智能体基线中，EnergyBridge在两个地区均实现了最高的模拟授权、最低的事件窗口能耗和最可靠的容量承诺。我们发布了人类数据和代码，用于可复现的以人为中心的电网灵活性研究：https://github.com/Agentic-Intelligence-Lab/EnergyBridge。

Original abstract: Residential virtual power plants (VPPs) can provide grid flexibility by shifting household demand, but physical flexibility becomes dependable capacity only when residents authorize a plan and the promised response is delivered. Existing benchmarks evaluate control but omit event-specific authorization. We present EnergyBridge, a benchmark and agent framework connecting capacity reporting, household authorization, and physical execution. It combines region-specific EnergyPlus environments for Tianjin and Berlin with an LLM-based User Participation Simulator. Against 584 persona- and event-matched human role-play judgments, the LLM-based User Participation Simulator preserves method ordering with a 5.3-point mean absolute acceptance error. Across conventional controllers and agent baselines, EnergyBridge achieves the highest simulated authorization, lowest event-window energy, and the most reliable capacity commitment in both regions. We release human data and codes for reproducible human-centered grid-flexibility research: https://github.com/Agentic-Intelligence-Lab/EnergyBridge.

</details>


### [64] [PluginEval: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Fine-Grained Error Attribution in Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08700)
*Dongjie Xu,Julius,Hanchi Dong,Minghua Tang,Yuxuan Sun,Ziwei Nie,Zicheng Liu,Dujun Qing,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 构建函数调用错误归因诊断基准，面向工具路由与agent可靠性评测，与coding agent直接相关

TL;DR: 本文提出PluginEval，一个通过两阶段闭环框架构建的工具路由基准，利用真实API执行验证并生成对抗性负样本，以更可靠地区分模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在数据分布幂律导致稀有场景稀疏、缺少对抗性负样本难以区分模型、以及标注依赖未经验证的LLM判断三个问题。

Method: 将工具路由分解为三个决策并分离生成与验证；按能力、意图和边界分解插件以生成多难度查询和对抗负样本；两阶段闭环迭代直至覆盖收敛；用锚定黄金标注的LLM评判者分类失败类型。

Result: 评估了五个模型家族（专有和开放权重），分析不同难度和错误类别的表现，并通过与人类标注的一致性验证评判者。

Conclusion: PluginEval提供了更可靠的工具路由评估方法，通过结构化生成和验证闭环弥补了现有基准的不足。

中文摘要：随着大型语言模型越来越多地作为自主代理运行，工具路由的可靠评估变得至关重要。当前的基准测试面临三个结构性局限：遵循幂律分布的数据分布使得稀有场景代表性不足；缺乏对抗性硬负样本掩盖了不同模型之间的性能差异；以及标注流程依赖于未经执行验证的LLM判断。在本文中，我们介绍了PluginEval，这是一个通过两阶段框架构建的基准测试，系统地缓解了这些局限。首先，我们将工具路由表述为三个决策的序列，并将生成与验证分离。LLM提出候选调用，而确定性验证和真实API执行提供可靠的质量信号。其次，我们按能力、意图和边界分解每个插件，以识别触发场景和排除场景。然后，我们生成不同难度级别的查询以填补覆盖空白，包括针对三种失败模式的对抗性负样本，并将其返回第一阶段进行标注。这个过程形成了一个闭环，迭代直到覆盖收敛。在评估方面，我们超越了总体准确率。一个锚定到黄金标注的LLM评判者将失败分类为遗漏调用、虚假调用或参数错误，为每个模型生成详细的错误剖析。我们评估了五个模型家族，包括专有模型和开放权重模型，分析了它们在难度级别和错误类别上的表现，并通过与人类标注的一致性验证了评判者。

Original abstract: Reliable evaluation of tool routing is critical as Large Language Models increasingly operate as autonomous agents. Current benchmarks face three structural limitations: data distributions that follow a power law leave rare scenarios underrepresented; the absence of adversarial hard negatives obscures performance differences across models; and annotation pipelines depend on LLM judgments that have not been validated through execution. In this paper, we introduce PluginEval, a benchmark constructed through a two-stage framework that systematically mitigates these limitations. First, we formulate tool routing as a sequence of three decisions and separate generation from verification. LLMs propose candidate calls, while deterministic validation and real API execution provide reliable quality signals. Second, we decompose each plugin by capability, intent, and boundary to identify trigger and exclusion scenarios. We then generate queries at different difficulty levels to fill coverage gaps, including adversarial negatives targeting three failure modes, and return them to the first stage for annotation. This process creates a closed loop that iterates until coverage converges. For evaluation, we move beyond aggregate accuracy. An LLM judge anchored to gold annotations classifies failures as missed calls, spurious calls, or parameter errors, producing a detailed error profile for each model. We evaluate five model families, including proprietary models and models with open weights, analyze their performance across difficulty levels and error categories, and validate the judge through agreement with human annotations.

</details>


### [65] [SymDiag: Explainable Diagnosis for LLM Reasoning via Neuro-Symbolic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08786)
*Wenyao Cui,Huaping Zhang,Yongyi Huang,Qiuchi Li,Jian Xu,Cheng-Lin Liu,Chunxiao Gao,Juan Wang,Baohua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

DOI: [10.1145/3770855.3818004](https://doi.org/10.1145/3770855.3818004)

Author affiliations: Wenyao Cui: Beijing Academy of Science and Technology; Huaping Zhang: Xinjiang Institute of Engineering; Yongyi Huang: Beijing Institute of Technology; Qiuchi Li: Beijing Institute of Technology; Jian Hui Xu: Shandong Institute of Automation; Cheng‐Lin Liu: Shandong Institute of Automation; Chunxiao Gao: Beijing Institute of Technology; Juan Wang: Beijing Institute of Technology; Baohua Zhang: Beijing Institute of Technology

Selection reason: 神经符号验证定位LLM推理失败步骤并提供可验证反馈，可迁移到coding agent的推理验证与修复

TL;DR: SymDiag是一个神经符号框架，通过将思维链转换为符号约束并执行逐步可满足性检查，实现结构化失败诊断，能区分翻译错误与推理错误，比现有验证方法更有效地检测不忠实推理并提供修复反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理验证信号缺乏诊断性：答案匹配只看结果，LLM评判主观且不可验证，标量奖励无法定位多步推导中的失败步骤。

Method: SymDiag将自然语言思维链翻译为符号约束，进行逐步可满足性/蕴含检查以定位失败步骤并生成证据（反例、不一致见证、缺失前提指示），并引入Self-Auditor通过双重符号编码一致性检查区分翻译误差和推理误差。

Result: 在数学、逻辑、科学和通用推理基准上，SymDiag改进了不忠实推理的检测，并比结果验证和LLM评判提供更有效的多轮推理修复反馈。

Conclusion: SymDiag为可信且可扩展的推理诊断提供了原则性基础，展示了神经符号方法在推理验证中的价值。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地充当数据驱动的推理器，然而它们的思维链（CoT）即使最终答案正确，也可能是不忠实的。大多数现有的“验证”信号不具备诊断性：答案匹配只观察结果，LLM即评判者提供主观且不可验证的批评，而标量奖励（如PRM/RM）对多步推导的失败位置几乎无法提供洞察。我们提出SymDiag，一个神经符号框架，将推理验证重新构建为结构化失败诊断。SymDiag将自然语言思维链转换为符号约束，并执行逐步的可满足性/蕴含检查，以（i）定位失败步骤，并（ii）生成可验证的诊断证据，包括反例、不一致见证和缺失前提指示。一个核心挑战是，表面上的“逻辑违规”可能由真正的推理缺陷或神经到符号的翻译噪声引起。因此，SymDiag包含一个自我审计器，通过双重符号编码一致性检查来区分翻译错误和推理错误，从而在部分可观测性下实现稳健诊断。在多种数学、逻辑、科学和通用推理基准上，SymDiag比仅基于结果的验证和基于LLM的评判更能改进对不忠实推理的检测，并为多轮推理修复提供显著更有效的反馈，为可信且可扩展的推理诊断提供了原则性基础。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as data-driven reasoners, yet their chains-of-thought (CoT) can be unfaithful even when final answers are correct. Most existing ``verification'' signals are not diagnostic: answer matching observes only the outcome, LLM-as-judge provides subjective and non-verifiable critiques, and scalar rewards (e.g., PRMs/RMs) offer little insight into where a multi-step derivation fails.We propose \textbf{SymDiag}, a neuro-symbolic framework that \textbf{reframes reasoning verification as structured failure diagnosis}. SymDiag translates natural-language CoT into symbolic constraints and performs step-level satisfiability/entailment checks to (i) localize failing steps and (ii) produce verifiable diagnostic evidence, including counterexamples, inconsistency witnesses, and missing-premise indicators. A central challenge is that apparent ``logic violations'' can be caused either by genuine reasoning defects or by neural-to-symbolic translation noise. SymDiag therefore incorporates a Self-Auditor that disentangles TranslationError from ReasoningError via dual symbolic encodings consistency checks, enabling robust diagnosis under partial observability. Across diverse mathematical, logical, scientific, and general reasoning benchmarks, SymDiag improves detection of unfaithful reasoning and provides substantially more effective feedback for multi-round reasoning repair than outcome-only verification and LLM-based judging, offering a principled foundation for trustworthy and scalable reasoning diagnosis.

</details>


### [66] [Improving Generalization Robustness of Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08802)
*Pengfei Zhou,Zhiwei Tang,Xiaopeng Peng,Chenrui Zhou,Lama Moukheiber,Yixing Ma,Bin Xu,Jiajun Song,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Jiasheng Tang,Bohan Zhuang,Fan Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 32 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 通过动态三值奖励与一致性正则增强多模态RLVR鲁棒性，奖励设计与策略不变性对agent训练有启发

TL;DR: 针对RLVR在提示扰动下性能脆弱的问题，提出PIRL（提示不变RLVR），通过动态三值奖励和嵌入空间对抗一致性正则化，将基准测试性能下降从GRPO的约3%降至≤1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR的二元奖励混淆格式与内容，且训练分布覆盖不足，导致模型在面对改写问题或变换提示模板时性能显著下降，难以在高风险场景（如医疗VQA）中可靠部署。

Method: PIRL包含两个关键组件：动态三值奖励（区分格式错误、语义错误和正确回答）和基于嵌入空间对抗器的一致性正则化器（强制模型对语义等价的扰动提示产生不变策略）。

Result: 在压力测试下，PIRL在基准测试上的平均准确率仅下降≤1%，而GRPO下降约3%；在动态评估中，PIRL也实现了最小的性能下降。

Conclusion: PIRL通过分离格式与语义奖励并施加提示不变性，显著增强了RLVR的鲁棒性，在提示扰动下保持高性能，为高风险场景部署提供了更可靠的方案。

中文摘要：具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）使多模态大语言模型更加准确，但这种增益是脆弱的：简单地改写问题或更改提示模板就可能使其性能下降，这给医疗视觉问答等高风险场景中的可靠部署带来了挑战。我们将此追溯到标准强化学习目标的两个问题。首先，二元验证器将格式与内容混为一谈，因此奖励信号无法区分错误答案与格式错误的答案。其次，训练分布仅覆盖模型在部署时可能遇到的真实世界提示的很小一部分，因此在训练分布上表现良好的策略在测试时面对未见过的提示可能会表现不同。这两种失败都要求一种稳健的后训练方法，帮助策略覆盖更广泛的语义等价提示分布，我们确定了有助于实现这一目标的两项措施：在奖励中区分格式与语义，以及在具有等价语义的扰动提示之间应用策略不变性。因此，我们提出了提示不变强化学习（PIRL），由动态三值奖励和基于嵌入空间对抗器的一致性正则化器组成。在压力测试下，PIRL在基准测试上的平均准确率仅下降≤1%，而GRPO下降约3%。在动态评估中，PIRL也实现了最小的性能下降。

Original abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) makes Multimodal Large Language Models more accurate, but the gains are brittle: simply paraphrasing a question or changing the prompt template can degrade them, which challenges reliable deployment in high-stakes scenarios like medical VQA. We trace this to two issues of the standard RL objective. First, the binary verifier conflates format with content, so the reward signal cannot tell a wrong answer apart from a misformatted one. Second, the training distribution covers only a thin slice of the real-world prompts that the model might meet at deployment, so policies that perform well on the training distribution can behave differently under unseen prompts during test. Both failures call for a robust post-training method that helps the policy cover a broader distribution of semantically equivalent prompts, and we identify two measures that help achieve this objective: separating format from semantics in the reward, and applying policy invariance across perturbed prompts with equivalent semantics. We therefore propose Prompt-Invariant RLVR (PIRL), consisting of a dynamic trinary reward and a consistency regularizer based on an embedding-space adversary. Under stress testing, PIRL's average accuracy on benchmarks drops by only $\le 1\%$, where GRPO drops ~3%. On dynamic evaluation, PIRL also achieves the smallest performance drop.

</details>


### [67] [Findings of the First Teaching Monster Challenge: A Benchmark of Pedagogical Content Knowledge in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08852)
*Yi-Cheng Lin,Yu-Kai Guo,Szu-Chi Chen,Bo-Han Feng,Yun-Man Hsu,Hsiang Hsieh,Yu-Jung Lin,Yue-Ling Wu,Jia-Kai Dong,An-Yu Cheng,Yu-Han Huang,Lok-Lam Ieong,Kuan-Yu Chen,Ming-Douo Tchouang,Shao-Hua Sun,Che Lin,Jian-Jiun Ding,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: AI agent教学视频生成基准，揭示LLM-judge在强模型排序上的局限，对agent自动评测有一般参考价值

TL;DR: 提出了第一个以学习者角色为评估标准的教学视频生成基准“教学怪物挑战”，发现现有系统内容生成强但适应性弱，且LLM裁判对顶尖系统的区分度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理能力虽强，但缺乏对其教学内容知识（PCK）的基准测试，即能否根据特定学习者调整教学。

Method: 构建包含主题和学习者角色的教学视频生成基准，采用LLM裁判初筛、众包成对投票排名、专家小组终审的流程。

Result: 系统内容生成良好但呈现与适应能力弱；LLM裁判能识别尾部弱系统，但顶尖系统得分相近，排名与人类偏好不一致。

Conclusion: 需要同时改进教学生成系统和自动裁判，并发布基准、评分规则及人类判断以推动该领域发展。

中文摘要：AI代理现在可以解决问题、像领域专家一样回答，并生成多模态长内容。然而，它们能否根据指定的学习者调整课程，即教育领域所称的“教学内容知识”（PCK），尚未被基准化测试。为了衡量这一点，我们引入了“教学怪物挑战”（Teaching Monster Challenge），这是第一个将学习者角色作为明确评估标准的教学视频生成基准。每个系统被给予一个主题和一个学习者角色，并必须生成一个完整的教学视频。每个视频由LLM裁判筛选，通过众包成对投票排名，并由专家小组最终确定。第一版显示，当今的系统在处理内容方面表现良好，但在呈现内容以及使其适应学习者方面要薄弱得多。同一过程也暴露了自动评判的局限性。LLM裁判能够区分出明显的低性能尾部，但对最强系统的排名却表现不佳。最强系统从裁判那里获得的分数几乎相同，因此裁判对它们的排名与人类偏好不符。因此，进步不仅需要更好的教学系统，还需要更好的自动裁判，我们发布了基准、评分标准和人类判断作为两者的测试平台。

Original abstract: AI agents can now solve problems, answer like subject experts, and generate long-form multimodal content. However, whether they can adapt a lesson to fit a specified learner, which education calls Pedagogical Content Knowledge (PCK), has not been benchmarked. To measure it, we introduce the Teaching Monster Challenge, the first instructional video generation benchmark to treat the learner persona as an explicit evaluation criterion. Each system is given a topic and a learner persona and must generate a complete instructional video. Every video is screened by an LLM-judge, ranked by crowd pairwise voting, and finalized by an expert panel. The first edition shows that today's systems handle the content well but are far weaker at presenting it and adapting it to the learner. The same process exposes a limit of automatic judging. The LLM-judge separates a clear low-performing tail but ranks the strongest systems poorly. The strongest systems receive nearly identical scores from the judge, so its ranking of them does not match human preference. Progress therefore requires not only better teaching systems but also better automatic judges, and we release the benchmark, rubric, and human judgments as a testbed for both.

</details>


### [68] [LLM Reasoning for Subjective Tasks: Failure Modes, Mitigation, and Dynamic Reasoning Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08889)
*Juncheng Dong,Ding Tong,Ishan Gupta,Yuyan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20th ACM Conference on Recommender Systems (RecSys 2026)

Selection reason: 研究LLM在主观验证任务中的推理失败模式与RLVR导致的推理崩溃，提出条件长度惩罚与推理路由，可用于agent验证器设计。

TL;DR: 本研究揭示标准RLVR在主观验证任务中导致推理崩溃，提出条件长度惩罚算法和角色路由架构来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为主观验证器时，现有RLVR过度偏向数学推理，难以泛化到人类偏好，且发现推理崩溃现象。

Method: 提出条件长度惩罚的后训练算法，结合验证准确率与有界推理长度，并引入基于社会语言角色的中训练架构。

Result: 算法阻止崩溃并恢复性能；在1500个合成角色中，推理角色导致宏F1波动近0.38。

Conclusion: 提供可扩展算法补丁和长期架构蓝图，以对齐推理模型与主观约束。

中文摘要：推荐系统因个性化而蓬勃发展，其中“正确性”很少是二元真值，而是主观人类偏好的问题。当大型语言模型（LLMs）被部署为安全性和质量指南的自主验证器时，它们面临一个独特的挑战：上下文相关的偏好对齐。最近在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）方面的进展主要基于客观的数学任务。通过一项大规模研究，涵盖专有和开源模型，针对来自生产推荐平台的四个真实世界验证任务，我们探究显式推理是否能够泛化到主观的、以人为中心的行业准则。我们暴露了一个根本性的漏洞：僵化的、以数学为中心的推理轨迹实际上会降低验证性能，而应用标准RLVR会触发一种我们称之为“推理崩溃”的现象，其中策略放弃深思熟虑而转向快速启发式猜测。我们引入了一种条件长度惩罚的后训练算法，将验证准确性与有界推理长度交织在一起，阻止崩溃并恢复性能。最后，我们表明推理轨迹的有效性与其社会语言框架紧密相关：在1500个合成角色中，验证准确率仅根据所采用的推理角色就波动近0.38个宏F1——这证明了大部分主观验证错误实际上是推理风格不匹配。这一观察激发了一种中训练架构，通过上下文对齐的角色来路由推理。这项工作既提供了一个可扩展的算法补丁，也为使推理模型与真实世界主观约束对齐提供了长期架构蓝图。

Original abstract: Recommendation systems thrive on personalization, where ''correctness'' is rarely a binary truth but a matter of subjective human preference. As Large Language Models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous verifiers of safety and quality guidelines, they face a distinctive challenge: context-aware preference alignment. Recent gains in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are indexed mostly on objective, mathematical tasks. Through a large-scale study spanning both proprietary and open-source models on four real-world verification tasks from a production recommender platform, we ask whether explicit reasoning generalizes to subjective, human-centric industry rubrics. We expose a fundamental vulnerability: rigid, math-centric reasoning traces actively degrade verification, and applying standard RLVR triggers a phenomenon we term reasoning collapse, in which the policy abandons deliberation in favor of rapid heuristic guessing. We introduce a conditional length-penalized post-training algorithm that intertwines verification accuracy with bounded reasoning length, halting collapse and recovering performance. Finally, we show that a reasoning trace's efficacy is tightly coupled with its socio-linguistic framing: across 1500 synthesized personas, verification accuracy swings by nearly 0.38 macro-F1 depending solely on the adopted reasoning persona---evidence that much subjective-verification error is really reasoning-style mismatch. This observation motivates a mid-training architecture that routes reasoning through contextually aligned personas. This work offers both a scalable algorithmic patch and a long-term architectural blueprint for aligning reasoning models with real-world subjective constraints.

</details>


### [69] [Not an A11y: How Android Accessibility Exposes Mobile AI Agents to Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08939)
*Rahul Deivasigamani,Sayeda Faatin Alvi,Derqui Andrea,Kaushal Punjabi,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 揭示移动AI智能体因依赖无障碍元数据而面临间接提示注入的漏洞，提出攻击分类与零信任上下文隔离，对agent安全控制有通用启发。

TL;DR: 本文揭示了移动智能体框架因依赖未消毒的无障碍元数据和视觉输入而面临间接提示注入攻击，并评估了两种框架的攻击成功率，提出了安全架构改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在移动设备上执行复杂任务时，其架构依赖无障碍树和截图等未经验证的环境输入，这可能被恶意文本利用，导致智能体偏离原始目标并执行未授权操作。

Method: 通过构造对抗性提示，在视觉隐藏和完全暴露的攻击场景中测试MobileRun和Mobile-Use框架，测量目标劫持、上下文漂移和未授权操作的成功率。

Result: MobileRun与Gemma4:31B组合的攻击成功率为0.822，Mobile-Use与Qwen3.6:35B组合降至0.150，但上下文漂移和未授权操作仍存在。

Conclusion: 当前移动智能体框架缺乏语义上下文隔离，应将环境文本视为不可信数据，需要采用零信任输入验证、专用安全代理和严格上下文隔离来加强安全性。

中文摘要：自主AI智能体的崛起代表了用户与移动设备交互方式的一次重大范式转变。诸如MobileRun和Mobile-Use之类的框架能够自主导航Android应用程序并执行复杂的多步任务。为了解释用户界面，这些框架主要依赖Android无障碍（A11y）树，其次依赖视觉截图。在本文中，我们证明了这种对未消毒的无障碍元数据以及视觉输入的结构性依赖，引入了对间接提示注入的系统性漏洞。我们表明，对抗性提示可以导致自主智能体放弃其原始目标、违反上下文边界并执行未经授权的设备操作。我们的实证评估展示了在视觉隐藏和完全暴露的攻击场景下的目标劫持、上下文漂移和未经授权的操作。总体上，MobileRun在使用Gemma4:31B时达到了0.822的攻击成功率，而Mobile-Use与Qwen3.6:35B搭配使用时将此降至0.150，但并未消除上下文漂移或未经授权的操作。这些发现表明，当前的移动智能体框架未能强制语义上下文边界，将被动的环境文本视为可信指令。最后，我们提出了这些攻击的分类法，并讨论了在移动智能体架构中实施零信任输入验证、专用安全代理和严格上下文隔离的必要性。

Original abstract: The rise of autonomous AI agents represents a major paradigm shift in how users interact with mobile devices. Frameworks such as MobileRun and Mobile-Use can autonomously navigate Android applications and execute complex multi-step tasks. To interpret user interfaces, these frameworks rely primarily on Android accessibility (A11y) trees and secondarily on visual screenshots. In this paper, we demonstrate that this architectural dependence on unsanitized accessibility metadata, together with visual input, introduces a systemic vulnerability to indirect prompt injection. We show that adversarial prompts can cause autonomous agents to abandon their original objectives, violate context boundaries, and perform unauthorized device actions. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates goal hijacking, context drift, and unauthorized actions across visually hidden and fully exposed attack scenarios. In aggregate, MobileRun reaches an attack success rate of 0.822 with Gemma4:31B, while Mobile-Use with Qwen3.6:35B reduces this to 0.150 but does not eliminate context drift or unauthorized actions. These findings reveal that current mobile agent frameworks fail to enforce semantic context boundaries, treating passive environmental text as trusted instructions. Finally, we present a taxonomy of these attacks and discuss the need for zero-trust input validation, dedicated security agents, and strict context isolation within mobile agent architectures.

</details>


### [70] [Reading is not Reasoning: Bridging the Agentic Policy Gap in Vision-Text Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08960)
*Cheng Fan,Junyi Zhou,Tingzhang Luo,RongJian Xu,Qiyanhui Lu,Mingjian Zhu,Hanting Chen,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 针对视觉-文本压缩下agent能力下降的问题，提出跨模态策略自蒸馏框架CAPS，直接改进agent长程执行中的上下文压缩与策略保持。

TL;DR: 提出CAPS跨模态策略自蒸馏框架，通过文本历史策略监督视觉历史策略，在压缩历史成本的同时缩小能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-文本压缩虽能降低上下文成本，但导致智能体能力显著下降，且这种差距不能仅由OCR质量解释，存在智能体策略差距。

Method: CAPS采用两阶段自蒸馏：离线轨迹自蒸馏将文本策略的成功行为迁移到视觉历史输入，在线策略自蒸馏在强化学习中对视觉历史策略访问的状态提供密集监督。

Result: 在SearchQA上较AgentOCR提升5.0%（3B）和3.4%（7B），在ALFWorld上提升15.6%和14.5%，平均内存成本降低63.3%，峰值成本降低83.4%。

Conclusion: 显式跨模态策略自蒸馏能在视觉-文本压缩下保持智能体能力，同时大幅降低上下文成本。

中文摘要：多步语言模型智能体反复处理不断增长的交互相处历史，导致大量的上下文成本。视觉-文本压缩通过将历史渲染为图像来降低这些成本，但由此产生的模态转换造成了显著的能力差距。通过对历史恢复、匹配状态决策和完整轨迹的受控评估，我们表明这种差距不能仅用OCR质量来解释。视觉历史智能体在动作选择、查询制定、停止和证据使用方面表现出系统性漂移，揭示了智能体策略差距。我们引入了CAPS，一个两阶段的跨模态智能体策略自蒸馏框架，利用同一模型更强的文本历史策略来监督其视觉历史对应部分。离线轨迹自蒸馏将成功的文本策略行为迁移到视觉历史输入上，而在线策略自蒸馏在强化学习过程中为视觉历史策略所访问的状态提供密集监督。在SearchQA上，CAPS在3B和7B骨干网络上分别比AgentOCR提高了5.0%和3.4%。在完整历史的ALFWorld上，相应的增益分别为15.6%和14.5%。在各种设置下，与匹配的文本历史策略相比，CAPS平均内存上下文成本降低了高达63.3%，峰值成本降低了高达83.4%。这些结果表明，显式的跨模态策略自蒸馏可以在视觉-文本压缩下保持智能体能力。我们的代码将在未来版本中公开提供。

Original abstract: Multi-step language-model agents repeatedly process growing interaction histories, leading to substantial context costs. Vision--text compression reduces these costs by rendering history as images, but the resulting modality shift creates a marked capability gap. Through controlled evaluations of history recovery, matched-state decisions, and complete trajectories, we show that this gap cannot be explained by OCR quality alone. Visual-history agents exhibit systematic drift in action selection, query formulation, stopping, and evidence use, revealing an agentic policy gap. We introduce \textbf{CAPS}, a two-stage \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{A}gentic \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{S}elf-distillation framework that uses the same model's stronger text-history policy to supervise its visual-history counterpart. Offline trajectory self-distillation transfers successful text-policy behavior to visual-history inputs, while online policy self-distillation provides dense supervision on states visited by the visual-history policy during reinforcement learning. On SearchQA, CAPS improves over AgentOCR by 5.0\% and 3.4\% with 3B and 7B backbones, respectively. On full-history ALFWorld, the corresponding gains are 15.6\% and 14.5\%. Across settings, CAPS reduces average memory-context cost by up to 63.3\% and peak cost by up to 83.4\% relative to matched text-history policies. These results show that explicit cross-modal policy self-distillation can preserve agent capability under vision--text compression. Our code will be made publicly available in a future release.

</details>


### [71] [Context Is Not Authority: Structured Runtime Governance for Financial Market Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09025)
*Rui Tang,Qiangqiang Liu,Yichi Zhang,Youwei Wang,Xi Chen,Chen Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables. Qiangqiang Liu and Yichi Zhang are corresponding authors

Selection reason: 提出面向金融agent的结构化运行时治理契约SAGE-Fin，将效果授权与上下文边界控制用于agent可靠执行，可迁移到coding agent的副作用控制。

TL;DR: SAGE-Fin 提出一种金融专用权威交接合约，将运行时控制从文本扩展到实际效果，在测试与真实部署中展现出可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融代理即使拥有正确的上下文也可能产生未授权的实际效果（如客户承诺、交易或策略部署），需要一种针对效果而非仅文本的运行时控制机制。

Method: SAGE-Fin 将提案编译为类型化、适配器绑定的候选；记录缺失或过时的机构义务为覆盖债务；基于当前市场、账户、政策和对话状态来授予权限；要求名义类型与消费端匹配的精确工件收据；并规定证据和工作流进展不能替代效果权威，状态变化后需重新检查授权。

Result: 在 616 案例目录上，5 个确定性规范产生 3080 个输出，标签隔离测试获得 616/616 参考原型一致性（含 3/3 响应门固定装置）和 22 条路径测试；真实部署获得操作团队与最终用户的强烈正面反馈；3 个去标识化失败案例独立确认 0/3 被拦截，揭示了重复输出漂移等问题但未估计流行率。

Conclusion: SAGE-Fin 实现了可执行的合规性与实际工作流适配性，但当前结果只能证明一致性而非独立安全准确性，仍需更广泛的现场验证。

中文摘要：金融代理可能将正确的上下文转化为未授权的效果：面向客户的承诺、交易或已部署的政策。我们提出SAGE-Fin，一种金融专用的权威交接合约，它将拟议的效果（而非仅仅是其文本）作为运行时控制的对象。SAGE-Fin将提案编译为类型化、绑定适配器的候选对象；将缺失或过时的机构义务记录为覆盖债务；在当前市场、账户、政策和对话状态下签订权威合约；并要求一份精确工件收据，其名义类型与消费响应、执行或策略适配器匹配。证据和工作流进展不能替代效果权威，并且在状态变化后重新检查先前授权。在一个人工编写的616案例目录中，五个确定性规范产生3,080个输出；一个标签隔离的测试平台获得616/616的二元参考原型一致性，包括3/3的命名响应门固定装置，同时22个测试覆盖选定路径。这些结果建立了可执行的合规性，而非独立的安全准确性。另外，SAGE-Fin的响应门在一个保密的数字资产平台上处理了真实的面向客户的生产请求。一个独立于实施团队的操作团队在部署后对实际实用性和工作流适配性得出了强烈积极的结论，最终用户反馈也非常积极。披露仅允许审查的独立性、利益相关者类别、评估维度和方向性结论，因此这是定性现场佐证，而非总体效果估计。三个不同的去标识化先前失败案例，经独立确认0/3拦截，奠定了重复输出漂移、过时账户证据和缺失升级状态的基础，但未估计流行率或治疗效果。

Original abstract: Financial agents can turn correct context into an unauthorized effect: a customer-facing commitment, trade, or deployed policy. We present SAGE-Fin, a finance-specific authority-handoff contract that makes the proposed effect, not merely its text, the object of runtime control. SAGE-Fin compiles proposals into typed, adapter-bound candidates; records missing or stale institutional obligations as coverage debt; contracts authority under current market, account, policy, and dialogue state; and requires an exact-artifact receipt whose nominal type matches the consuming response, execution, or policy adapter. Evidence and workflow progress cannot substitute for effect authority, and prior authorization is rechecked after state changes. Across an authored 616-case catalog, five deterministic specifications yield 3,080 outputs; a label-isolated harness obtains 616/616 binary reference-prototype parity, including 3/3 named response-gate fixtures, while 22 tests cover selected paths. These results establish executable conformance, not independent safety accuracy. Separately, SAGE-Fin's response gate processed real customer-facing production requests at a confidential digital-asset platform. An operational team independent of the implementation team reached a strongly positive post-deployment conclusion on practical usefulness and workflow fit, and end-user feedback was also strongly positive. Disclosure permits only the review's independence, stakeholder classes, assessed dimensions, and directional conclusion, so this is qualitative field corroboration rather than an aggregate effect estimate. Three distinct de-identified predecessor failures, with independently confirmed 0/3 interception, ground repeated-emission drift, stale account evidence, and missing escalation state without estimating prevalence or treatment effect.

</details>


### [72] [PolicyKG: An Agentic LLM Pipeline for Translating Institutional Policies into SHACL Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09028)
*Ponkrit Kaewsawee,Chaklam Silpasuwanchai,Chutiporn Anutariya*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Under review at IJCKG 2026, Bangkok, Thailand

Selection reason: PolicyKG用LangGraph状态机与分阶段验证器将自然语言政策转为SHACL约束，属于通用agent工作流与结构化输出验证方法。

TL;DR: PolicyKG 是一个 LLM 管道，通过语料库适配器和词汇注册表，将机构政策自动转换为 SHACL 约束，在多个基准上验证有效，且支持领域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构政策与合规系统之间存在语言鸿沟，人工桥接成本高且易错。PolicyKG 旨在自动化这一过程，并支持无需重新训练的领域迁移。

Method: PolicyKG 采用四阶段 LLM 管道：句子级道义分类、一阶道义逻辑提升、SHACL 约束生成，配合 LangGraph 状态机和逐阶段验证器。核心是 Corpus Adapter（YAML 词汇注册表），将 LLM 谓词映射到目标本体。

Result: 在 AIT 语料库上，道义分类准确率 86.9%（kappa=.709），标注者一致性 Fleiss' kappa=.844，SHACL 形状 F1=.866。FOL 路径覆盖 79.2% 规则，对 443 条规则的高阶构造审计未发现任何违规，HOL 率上限 0.67%。将注册表从 AIT 换成 GDPR 后，属性对齐从 1/15 提升至 11/15（p<.001）；但在 LexDeMod 上 Macro F1 降至 .370，归因于词汇不匹配。重复运行输出哈希一致。

Conclusion: PolicyKG 提供了自动化的策略合规约束生成，通过词汇注册表实现领域迁移，并展示了可验证的高阶构造审计结果；但 FOL 充分性仅针对单一语料库，仍需在更广泛领域验证。

中文摘要：机构政策以自然语言形式存在，而检查合规性的系统却需要机器可读的约束。弥合这一差距目前仍由人工完成。PolicyKG 闭合了这一循环。它是一个 LLM 管道，读取政策 PDF，将每个句子分类为义务、许可或禁止，将该标签提升为一阶道义逻辑，并生成 SHACL 约束。四个阶段在带有逐阶段验证器的 LangGraph 状态机上运行。其中最重要的部分是语料库适配器（Corpus Adapter）：一个 YAML 词汇注册表，将 LLM 谓词锚定到目标本体中。重新定位到新领域意味着只需更换注册表，而无需重新训练模型。在亚洲理工学院政策与程序语料库（1,663 个句子，443 条规则）上，PolicyKG 达到了 86.9% 的道义分类准确率（Cohen's kappa = .709）。三位标注者独立重新标注了一个 50 项样本，Fleiss' kappa = .844。在 69 个形状子集上的 SHACL 形状正确性为 F1 = .866。FOL 路径处理了 79.2% 的规则；其余规则通过直接的 NL-to-SHACL 回退处理。我们对全部 443 条规则审计了二阶或更高阶构造。自动正则表达式检查清单未标记任何规则，且第一作者对 92 个 FOL 回退案例的复查证实了同样结果。真实 HOL 率的 95% Clopper-Pearson 精确上限为 0.67%。这是针对一个语料库的审计发现，并非 FOL 对机构政策充分性的证明。将 AIT 注册表替换为 GDPR 注册表，可将精确属性对齐率从 1/15 提高到 11/15（Fisher 精确检验 p < .001；Cohen's h = 1.53）。在 LexDeMod 租赁合同基准（N = 200）上，Macro F1 降至 .370，因为租赁英语使用 'shall be entitled' 表示许可——这正是注册表替换旨在修复的词汇不匹配问题。重复运行产生哈希完全相同的 SHACL 输出。

Original abstract: Institutional policies stay in natural language while the systems that check compliance demand machine-readable constraints. Bridging that gap is still done by hand.
  PolicyKG closes the loop. It is an LLM pipeline that reads a policy PDF, classifies each sentence as an obligation, permission, or prohibition, lifts the label into first-order deontic logic, and emits SHACL constraints. Four stages run on a LangGraph state machine with per-stage validators. The piece that matters most is the Corpus Adapter: a YAML vocabulary registry that grounds LLM predicates in a target ontology. Retargeting to a new domain means swapping the registry, not retraining a model.
  On the Asian Institute of Technology Policies and Procedures corpus (1,663 sentences, 443 rules), PolicyKG reaches 86.9% deontic classification accuracy (Cohen's kappa = .709). Three annotators independently re-label a 50-item sample and agree at Fleiss' kappa = .844. SHACL shape correctness on a 69-shape subset is F1 = .866. The FOL path handles 79.2% of rules; the rest go through a direct NL-to-SHACL fallback.
  We audited every one of the 443 rules for second- or higher-order constructs. An automated regex checklist flagged none, and a first-author pass on the 92 FOL-fallback cases confirmed the same. The exact upper 95% Clopper-Pearson bound on the true HOL rate is 0.67%. This is an audit finding for one corpus, not a proof of FOL sufficiency for institutional policy.
  Swapping the AIT registry for a GDPR registry raises exact property alignment from 1/15 to 11/15 (Fisher's exact p < .001; Cohen's h = 1.53). On the LexDeMod lease-contract benchmark (N = 200), Macro F1 drops to .370 because lease English uses "shall be entitled" for permission -- exactly the vocabulary mismatch registry swap is meant to fix. Repeated runs produce hash-identical SHACL outputs.

</details>


### [73] [Different Feedback, Different Updates: Selective Self-Learning from User Interactions for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09109)
*Xuanchen Li,Haitao Li,Yujia Zhou,Qingyi Pan,Heng Wang,Yiqun Liu,Min Zhang,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: SLIFT将用户反馈分解为Fix/Spec/Null组件并通过双LoRA适配器实现选择性自我学习，可迁移到代码优化agent从反馈中持续改进。

TL;DR: 提出SLIFT框架，将用户反馈分解为Fix、Spec、Null三类，并通过Generalist和Specialist两个LoRA适配器在冻结骨干上分别整合不同粒度的改进，在MemoryBench和WildFB上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户反馈中单条消息可能包含多种行为变化，其泛化范围不同，需要将反馈分解并适配到合适的更新范围，以避免错误泛化或无效更新。

Method: SLIFT将反馈分解为原子组件，按任务相对视图分类为Fix（任务有效性要求）、Spec（条件特定细化）和Null（无可靠正向更新）。训练两个互补LoRA适配器：Generalist通过反馈条件的自蒸馏整合Fix到默认行为；Specialist仅基于任务和Generalist响应提供Spec残余指导。

Result: 在不同骨干网络上，SLIFT在MemoryBench和WildFB基准上均取得强劲性能，并通过针对性分析验证了机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SLIFT能有效利用用户反馈实现选择性、多粒度的LLM改进，且代码已开源，为后续研究提供基础。

中文摘要：用户反馈为持续改进LLM提供了自然的监督，但单条消息可能支持多种具有不同泛化范围的行为变化。我们引入了SLIFT，一种基于用户反馈的任务相对视图的选择性自学习框架。SLIFT将每条反馈消息分解为原子组件，并将每个组件相对于原始任务解释为Fix、Spec或Null：分别对应任务有效性的要求、兼容的条件特定细化、或没有可靠正向更新方向的内容。为了在适当的范围内纳入每个变化，SLIFT在共享的冻结骨干上训练两个互补的LoRA适配器：一个Generalist，通过反馈条件的自蒸馏将Fix要求整合到默认行为中；以及一个Specialist，仅观察任务和Generalist的响应，为适用但未满足的Spec细化提供残余指导。Null组件不诱导正向更新。在各种骨干网络上，SLIFT在MemoryBench和WildFB上均取得了强劲的性能，进一步的分析则检验了其底层机制。我们在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SLIFT发布代码。

Original abstract: User feedback offers natural supervision for persistent LLM improvement, but a single message may support multiple behavioral changes with different scopes of generalization. We introduce SLIFT, a selective self-learning framework built on a task-relative view of user feedback. SLIFT decomposes each feedback message into atomic components and interprets each component relative to the original task as Fix, Spec, or Null: requirements for task validity, compatible condition-specific refinements, or content with no reliable positive update direction. To incorporate each change at the appropriate scope, SLIFT trains two complementary LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone: a Generalist that consolidates Fix requirements into default behavior through feedback-conditioned self-distillation, and a Specialist that observes only the task and Generalist response to supply residual guidance for applicable, unmet Spec refinements. Null components induce no positive update. Across backbones, SLIFT achieves strong performance on both MemoryBench and WildFB, with targeted analyses further examining its underlying mechanisms. We release our code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SLIFT.

</details>


### [74] [MELLON - Multimodal Enhanced LLM for Online Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09121)
*Ruiyu Li,Haoyang Cai,Zhitong Guo,Tong Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: MELLON为WebShop导航agent引入多模态对齐与推理增强，是面向在线任务的多模态agent方法，对agent状态理解有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文针对WebShop基准提出三种多模态增强方法（MELLON、VQAgent、多模态排序器），其中MELLON仅训练一个周期即带来9.26%的准确率提升，表明多模态方法对Web导航代理的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Web导航代理基线要么是单模态的，要么在处理多模态输入时推理能力不足，限制了对真实网站模拟（如WebShop）中视觉与文本信息的利用，因此需要探索多模态对齐与推理能力来提升导航性能。

Method: 提出三种多模态增强方案：MELLON（多模态增强大语言模型，用于在线导航）、VQAgent（基于向量量化的智能体）以及多模态排序器；这些方法旨在对齐文本和图像特征，并增强导航过程中的推理与规划能力。

Result: 在WebShop基准上，MELLON在仅经过一个训练周期后，任务完成准确率提升了9.26%，验证了多模态增强的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，多模态方法（尤其是结合更广泛训练和对齐策略）是提升Web导航代理性能的重要方向，未来应进一步深入探索。

中文摘要：Web导航代理能够处理不同网站上的各种类型的任务。当前Web导航的基线要么是单模态的，要么在给定多模态输入时缺乏强大的推理能力。聚焦于WebShop基准（一个真实世界的网站模拟），我们探索了文本和图像的对齐，以及多模态推理和规划能力，以提升Web导航代理的性能。我们提出了三种创新的多模态增强方法：MELLON（用于在线导航的多模态增强大语言模型）、VQAgent和多模态排序器。MELLON在任务完成准确率上展现出显著提升，仅经过一个训练周期就提高了9.26%。我们的研究结果表明，有必要进一步探索多模态方法，重点关注更广泛的训练和对齐策略，以增强Web导航代理的有效性。

Original abstract: Web navigation agents are capable of addressing various types of tasks on different websites. Current baselines on web navigation are either unimodal or lack strong reasoning abilities given multimodal inputs. Focusing on the WebShop benchmark, a real-world website simulation, we explore the alignment of text and images, as well as multimodal reasoning and planning abilities, to enhance the performance of web navigation agents. We propose three innovative multimodal enhancements: Multimodal Enhanced LLM for Online Navigation (MELLON), VQAgent, and Multimodal Ranker. MELLON demonstrates a significant improvement in task completion accuracy, with a 9.26% increase after just one epoch of training. Our findings suggest the necessity of further exploration into multimodal approaches, with a focus on more extensive training and alignment strategies to enhance the effectiveness of web navigation agents.

</details>


### [75] [TRACE: TRajectory Attribution for Automated Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09153)
*Yikai Zhao,Pradeep Kumar Misra,Saurabh Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables

Selection reason: 从历史轨迹挖掘上下文故障并自动修复，对 agent 的上下文工程与自改进有直接价值。

TL;DR: TRACE是一个自动化上下文工程反馈循环，通过挖掘历史智能体轨迹中的隐含不满信号，诊断并修复上下文层故障，无需显式反馈或模型重训练，在60条轨迹上达到72.7%根因归因和82%端到端修复有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产AI智能体常因上下文来源（提示、知识库、工具描述、技能）中的错误或缺口而失败，而现有维护依赖手动日志审查和临时调试，无法随交互量扩展。缺乏自动化诊断和修复手段，且没有公开的上下文调试数据集。

Method: TRACE包含四个核心组件：（1）轨迹挖掘框架，从历史执行中提取诊断信息；（2）多组件因果归因，将文本梯度扩展到异构上下文来源；（3）探索性验证，通过主动阅读上下文来源区分内容缺口（CREATE）和过时内容（UPDATE）；（4）可复用的模拟方法论和基准，含六类故障分类、标注和跨层验证协议。

Result: 在60条不满轨迹（最多16个执行节点，三个复杂度层级）上，TRACE实现72.7%的根因归因准确率、96%的操作准确率（CREATE vs UPDATE）和82%的端到端修复有效性，证明超过80%的上下文层故障可自动诊断和修复。

Conclusion: 挖掘历史轨迹是一种未被充分利用的生产资源，能够有效自动诊断和修复上下文层故障，TRACE提供了一种无需重训练、可快速迭代的解决方案，为生产环境中的上下文工程提供了新范式。

中文摘要：生产环境中的AI智能体在其上下文来源——系统提示、知识库、工具描述和过程技能——包含错误或缺口时会失效。当前的维护依赖人工日志审查和临时调试，随着交互量的增长造成了可扩展性瓶颈。我们提出TRACE（轨迹归因用于自动化上下文工程），一种自动化反馈循环，通过挖掘历史智能体轨迹来诊断和修复上下文故障。我们的关键洞见是，轨迹中蕴含丰富的隐含不满信号——用户纠正、重新表述、放弃线索——这些信号无需显式反馈收集即可精确揭示上下文来源失败的位置。与模型微调不同，TRACE在上下文层操作，无需重新训练即可快速迭代。我们做出四项贡献：（1）一个轨迹挖掘框架，系统地从历史智能体执行中提取诊断信息；（2）多组件因果归因，将文本梯度从单一提示优化扩展到异构上下文来源（技能、知识库、工具、提示）；（3）探索性验证，其中智能体主动读取上下文来源以区分需要创建（CREATE）的内容缺口与需要更新（UPDATE）的过时内容，实现了96%的操作准确率；（4）一种可复用的模拟方法论和可验证基准，以应对上下文调试公开数据集的缺失，包含六类故障分类、真实标注和跨层验证协议。在跨越三个复杂度层级（最多16个执行节点）的60条不满轨迹上，TRACE实现了72.7%的根因归因准确率和82%的端到端修复有效性，表明通过挖掘历史轨迹（生产系统中被忽视的资源）可以自动诊断和修复超过80%的上下文层故障。

Original abstract: Production AI agents fail when their context sources -- system prompts, knowledge bases, tool descriptions, and procedural skills -- contain errors or gaps. Current maintenance relies on manual log review and ad-hoc debugging, creating a scalability bottleneck as interaction volume grows.
  We present TRACE (TRajectory Attribution for Automated Context Engineering), an automated feedback loop that mines historical agent trajectories to diagnose and remediate context failures. Our key insight is that trajectories are rich with implicit dissatisfaction signals -- user corrections, rephrasing, abandonment cues -- that reveal precisely where context sources failed, without explicit feedback collection. Unlike model fine-tuning, TRACE operates on the context layer, enabling rapid iteration without retraining.
  We make four contributions: (1) a trajectory mining framework that systematically extracts diagnostic information from historical agent executions; (2) multi-component causal attribution that extends textual gradients from monolithic prompt optimization to heterogeneous context sources (skills, knowledge bases, tools, prompts); (3) exploratory verification, where agents actively read context sources to distinguish content gaps requiring CREATE from stale content requiring UPDATE, achieving 96% operation accuracy; and (4) a reusable simulation methodology and verifiable benchmark addressing the absence of open datasets for context debugging, with a six-category fault taxonomy, ground truth annotations, and a cross-layer verification protocol.
  On 60 dissatisfaction traces spanning three complexity tiers (up to 16 execution nodes), TRACE achieves 72.7% root cause attribution and 82% end-to-end fix effectiveness, showing that over 80% of context-layer failures can be automatically diagnosed and remediated by mining historical trajectories, an overlooked resource in production systems.

</details>


### [76] [From Relevance to Execution Utility: Reward-Aware Dynamic Execution Gating for Skill-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09168)
*Liang He,Jingbo Wen,Hongyu Gu,Hao Li,Haoyu Wang,Yixiong Chen,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用代理模型预测技能执行效用并动态门控，提升技能型 agent 的效率与可靠性。

TL;DR: 提出RADEG，一个轻量级决策层，通过低成本替代模型预测技能组合执行效用，避免昂贵rollout，减少不必要执行且保留奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能检索结果不一定值得执行，且每次执行成本高昂，需要一种在检索后决定是否执行的方法。

Method: RADEG学习替代模型，通过局部扰动技能组合生成监督信号，部署时仅更新逻辑回归头以适应新反馈。

Result: 在288个rollouts上，大幅减少不必要执行，保留大部分奖励，且优于基线和随机门控。

Conclusion: 执行感知的替代建模是技能检索的实用且成本效益高的补充，能有效决定执行/跳过边界。

中文摘要：智能体技能越来越广泛地用于为大型语言模型（LLM）智能体配备可重用的程序性知识。尽管近期工作通过不断增长的技能库显著改善了技能检索，但检索到一个看似合理的技能组合并不能保证执行它是有价值的。由于每次技能条件化 rollout 的计算成本高昂，决定是否应执行检索到的组合已成为一个日益重要的挑战。为此，我们引入了奖励感知动态执行门（RADEG），这是一个位于技能检索与智能体执行之间的轻量级、检索器无关的决策层。RADEG 学习一个低成本替代模型，在昂贵的 rollout 启动之前预测查询-组合对的执行效用。为了在控制任务难度的同时获得有效的监督信号，我们对每个检索到的组合进行局部扰动，通过删除、添加或替换一个技能，生成匹配的同一查询 rollout，从而隔离技能组合对验证器奖励的影响。在部署期间，RADEG 仅更新一个热启动的逻辑回归头，利用新的验证器反馈进行低成本自适应，无需重新训练检索器或智能体即可调整执行/跳过边界。在包含 288 个收集的 rollouts 的查询级留出评估中，RADEG 大幅减少了不必要的智能体执行，同时保留了下游验证器奖励的很大一部分。它在不同执行预算下始终优于基于相关性的门控和随机门控，证明了执行感知的替代建模是技能检索的一种实用且成本效益高的补充。

Original abstract: Agent skills are increasingly used to equip large language model (LLM) agents with reusable procedural knowledge. Although recent work has substantially improved skill retrieval due to the increasing skill libraries, retrieving a plausible skill bundle does not guarantee that executing it is worthwhile. Since every skill-conditioned rollout is computationally expensive, deciding whether a retrieved bundle should be executed has become an increasingly important challenge. To this end, we introduce the Reward-Aware Dynamic Execution Gate (RADEG), a lightweight, retriever-agnostic decision layer between skill retrieval and agent execution. RADEG learns a low-cost surrogate model that predicts the execution utility of a query--bundle pair before the expensive rollout is launched. To obtain informative supervision while controlling for task difficulty, we locally perturb each retrieved bundle by deleting, adding, or replacing one skill, producing matched same-query rollouts that isolate the effect of bundle composition on verifier reward. During deployment, RADEG updates only a warm-started logistic head as new verifier feedback becomes available, enabling inexpensive adaptation of the execute/skip boundary without retraining either the retriever or the agent. Under a query-level held-out evaluation on 288 collected rollouts, RADEG substantially reduces unnecessary agent executions while preserving a large fraction of the downstream verifier reward. It consistently outperforms relevance-based and random gating across different execution budgets, demonstrating that execution-aware surrogate modeling provides a practical and cost-effective complement to skill retrieval.

</details>


### [77] [Agentic Router: An Execution-Grounded Continual Learning Approach With Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09184)
*Yuxuan Chen,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Yuntao Liu,Xing Xu,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 执行反馈驱动的双路径持续学习 agent，方法可迁移到编码 agent 的决策与工具使用。

TL;DR: 提出一种执行接地的双路径后果感知智能体，通过多动作生成、后果预测和效用/风险重排序，并利用可检索经验与SSH反馈的LoRA适应，提升CLI网络操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体进行命令行网络操作时，即使命令看似合理，执行后仍可能失败或带来风险；已有方法侧重命令生成或最终配置正确性，没有利用执行接地的经验来同时改进候选覆盖率和动作选择。

Method: 构建双路径后果感知智能体：提议侧将可复用操作经验抽象为可检索指导以提升可行动作覆盖率（不修改提议LLM）；选择侧使用真实SSH反馈进行会话级LoRA更新，适应后果预测器，并通过效用与风险感知重排序选出最终动作。

Result: 在不同Qwen3提议模型的多轮SONiC操作会话实验中，框架显著提升了可行动作覆盖率和top-1执行成功率，且两条适应路径提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合执行反馈与双路径适应机制，提升了CLI网络操作的安全性和成功率，验证了执行接地经验在候选生成与动作选择中的联合价值。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体为基于命令行的网络操作提供了一种有前途的接口，但一个看似合理的命令在执行后仍可能失败或引入操作风险。现有方法主要关注命令生成或最终配置的正确性，并未利用基于执行的经验来联合改进候选覆盖率和动作选择。我们提出了一种用于基于CLI的SONiC操作的执行接地双路径后果感知智能体，它生成多个完整动作，预测其执行后果，并通过效用与风险感知的重新排序来选择最终动作。提议侧路径将可复用的操作经验抽象为可检索的指导，以在不修改提议LLM的情况下提高可行动作覆盖率；选择侧路径则利用真实SSH反馈，通过会话级LoRA更新来适应后果预测器，以提高条件选择质量。在使用不同Qwen3提议模型的多轮SONiC操作会话上的实验表明，该框架提高了可行动作覆盖率和top-1执行成功率，并且两条适应路径在交互过程中提供了互补的增益。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents provide a promising interface for command-line-based network operations, but a plausible command may still fail or introduce operational risk after execution. Existing approaches mainly focus on command generation or final configuration correctness, and do not use execution-grounded experience to jointly improve candidate coverage and action selection. We propose an execution-grounded dual-path consequence-aware agent for CLI-based SONiC operations, which generates multiple complete actions, predicts their execution consequences, and selects the final action through utility- and risk-aware reranking. The proposal-side path abstracts reusable operational lessons into retrievable guidance to improve feasible-action coverage without modifying the proposal LLM, while the selection-side path adapts the consequence predictor through session-level LoRA updates using real SSH feedback to improve conditional selection quality. Experiments over multi-turn SONiC operation sessions with different Qwen3 proposal models show that the framework improves feasible-action coverage and top-1 execution success, and that the two adaptation paths provide complementary gains over interaction.

</details>


### [78] [Emotion2Skill: Model-Internal Emotion Signals for Adaptive Skill Selection and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09248)
*Bohan Lin,Hejia Geng,Xinyi Xie,Heng Zhou,Qinghua Xing,Bo Liu,Chen Zhang,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 利用 LLM 内部情感表示指导技能选择与演化，为 agent 决策提供新信号。

TL;DR: Emotion2Skill通过提取LLM内部27维情绪向量并注入路由提示，同时利用情绪轨迹突变定位问题技能以重写SOP，在WebShop和ALFWorld上显著提升技能智能体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能型LLM智能体仅依赖文本信号进行技能路由，忽略了模型内部表征状态；已有情绪表征研究只用于事后分析或输出引导，未用于智能体决策。

Method: 提出Emotion2Skill：每步从残差流提取27维情绪状态，经置信度门控摘要注入路由提示；并分析情绪轨迹的突变来识别问题技能调用，指导SOP重写。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld上，Qwen3-8B相比Zero-Shot分别提升+26.9%成功率和+25.5%平均成功率，且优于所有基线；Qwen3-14B上也有持续提升；共激活分析显示情绪-技能配对语义连贯。

Conclusion: LLM内部情绪表征可作为决策级信号用于编排智能体技能系统，超越可解释性和输出引导的用途，为技能选择与演化提供新途径。

中文摘要：基于技能的LLM智能体从外部库中选择可复用的流程来解决复杂任务，但其路由决策完全依赖于文本级信号，如任务描述、口头反思和经验推导的规则，而模型自身的内部表征状态仍未得到观测。最近的可解释性工作表明，LLM维持着线性情绪表征，这些表征对行为有因果影响；然而，这些表征仅被用于事后分析或直接输出引导，尚未被用于指导智能体层面的决策。我们提出Emotion2Skill，一个从LLM内部提取情绪向量并将其融入技能选择与技能演化的框架。在每个决策步骤，从残差流中提取27维情绪状态，并映射为经过置信度门控的摘要注入路由提示中。除在线选择外，还分析情绪轨迹中的突然内部状态转移，以定位有问题的技能调用，指导有针对性的SOP重写，取代先前方法粗糙的二元结果信号。在WebShop和ALFWorld上，使用Qwen3-8B的Emotion2Skill相比Zero-Shot基线分别提高了+26.9%的成功率和+25.5%的平均成功率，在两个基准上均优于所有基线，并在Qwen3-14B上保持一致提升。共激活分析进一步揭示了语义连贯的情绪-技能配对，确认路由改进反映了有意义的内部状态信号，而非不透明的统计相关性。这些结果确立了LLM内部情绪表征作为编排智能体技能系统的有效决策级信号，将其效用扩展到可解释性和输出引导之外。代码可在https://github.com/BoHan-LIN04/Emotion2Skill获取。

Original abstract: Skill-based LLM agents select reusable procedures from an external library to solve complex tasks, yet their routing decisions rely entirely on text-level signals such as task descriptions, verbal reflections, and experience-derived rules, while the model's own internal representational state remains unobserved. Recent interpretability work has shown that LLMs maintain linear emotion representations that causally influence behavior; however, these representations have been exploited only for post-hoc analysis or direct output steering, and have not been used to inform agent-level decision-making. We propose Emotion2Skill, a framework that extracts LLM-internal emotion vectors and incorporates them into both skill selection and skill evolution. At each decision step, a 27-dimensional emotion state is extracted from the residual stream and mapped to a confidence-gated summary injected into the routing prompt. Beyond online selection, emotion trajectories are analyzed for abrupt internal-state shifts to pinpoint problematic skill invocations, guiding targeted SOP rewriting that replaces the coarse binary outcome signal of prior methods. On WebShop and ALFWorld, Emotion2Skill with Qwen3-8B improves over the Zero-Shot baseline by +26.9% success rate and +25.5% average success respectively, outperforming all baselines on both benchmarks with consistent gains on Qwen3-14B. Co-activation analysis further reveals semantically coherent emotion--skill pairings, confirming that the routing improvements reflect meaningful internal-state signals rather than opaque statistical correlations. These results establish LLM-internal emotion representations as an effective decision-level signal for orchestrating agent skill systems, extending their utility beyond interpretability and output steering. The code is available at https://github.com/BoHan-LIN04/Emotion2Skill.

</details>


### [79] [SkillSentry: Reliable Skill Execution for LLM Agents via Runtime Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09253)
*You Lu,Xinyu Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 通过运行时保证和 DSL 提升技能执行稳定性，直接适用于可靠编码 agent。

TL;DR: 提出SkillSentry，一种基于DSL的运行时保障框架，通过结合技能文档与执行历史来监控和迭代优化技能执行，在15个技能上平均提升24.1%成功率并降低变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体即使拥有技能，执行仍不可靠，会因偏离程序或错误步骤而在类似任务或重复运行中失败，限制了实际可靠性。

Method: SkillSentry利用DSL表示运行时指导，初始化时结合技能规范与历史成功/失败轨迹的经验，包裹执行循环进行监控和引导，并用新轨迹迭代优化指导。

Result: 在两个LLM智能体（各配两个骨干模型）的15项技能上，平均任务成功率提升24.1%，且重复运行的变异性更低。

Conclusion: SkillSentry能有效提升LLM智能体技能执行的可靠性和稳定性。

中文摘要：LLM智能体日益配备通过多步推理和工具使用执行复杂任务的技能。尽管技能提供了可重用的程序性知识，智能体仍可能不可靠地执行它们。即使智能体已在技能指导下展示了完成任务的能力，由于偏离技能程序或错误执行单个步骤，它可能在类似任务或重复运行中无法一致地执行。这种不稳定性限制了LLM智能体的实际可靠性。为解决此问题，我们提出了SkillSentry，一种面向技能运行时保障框架，基于一种新的领域特定语言（DSL）表示技能执行的运行时指导。SkillSentry通过结合从相应技能文档中提取的技能规范与从历史成功和失败轨迹中挖掘的执行经验来初始化运行时指导。然后，它包裹智能体执行循环，在当前指导下监视和指导技能执行，同时使用新收集的轨迹迭代优化指导。我们在两个LLM智能体的15项技能上评估了SkillSentry，每个智能体配有两个骨干模型，即Claude Code与Claude-Haiku-4.5和Claude-Opus-4.6，以及Codex与GPT-5.2和GPT-5.4。我们的结果显示，SkillSentry平均将LLM智能体的任务成功率提高了24.1%，并且在重复运行中表现出更低的变异性。

Original abstract: LLM agents are increasingly equipped with skills to perform complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. Although skills provide reusable procedural knowledge, agents may still execute them unreliably. Even when an agent has demonstrated the capability to complete tasks under the guidance of a skill, it may fail to do so consistently across similar tasks or repeated runs due to deviations from the skill procedure or incorrect execution of individual steps. Such instability limits the practical reliability of LLM agents. To address this problem, we propose SkillSentry, a skill-oriented runtime assurance framework built upon a new domain-specific language (DSL) for representing runtime guidance for skill execution. SkillSentry initializes the runtime guidance by combining a skill specification extracted from the corresponding skill document with execution experience mined from historical successful and failed traces. It then wraps around the agent execution loop to monitor and guide skill execution under the current guidance, while iteratively refining the guidance using newly collected traces. We evaluate SkillSentry on 15 skills across two LLM agents, each paired with two backbone models, i.e., Claude Code with Claude-Haiku-4.5 and Claude-Opus-4.6, and Codex with GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.4. Our results show that SkillSentry improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 24.1% across skills, on average, while exhibiting lower variability across repeated runs.

</details>


### [80] [Business Truth, not SQL Accuracy: A Rule-Gated 7B Analytics Agent Outperforms a Direct-Prompted 32B Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09254)
*Morris Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 7 figures, 11 tables. Benchmark, code, run records, pre-registration and one-command reproduction: https://github.com/k-w-lee/query_proof

Selection reason: 规则门控 + 确定性校验的业务分析 agent，强调业务真实性而非 SQL 准确率，对 agent 可靠性设计有启发。

TL;DR: 提出WarehouseReliabilityBench基准和7B的QueryProof代理，通过规则和确定性检查提升业务真相率，但增益主要来自确定性层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM分析代理评估仅关注SQL语法正确性，无法捕捉生产中的真实失败模式（如歧义、拒答、错误数字），需要新的基准和方法。

Method: 构建400个冻结任务，一半正确响应为澄清/弃权/拒绝；QueryProof使用语义层和物理目录规则，并依赖执行后确定性检查门控答案。

Result: QueryProof比直接提示的32B基线提升+0.237业务真相率，成本降低71%；错误成功从0.754降至0.351，但路由层贡献小，增益主要来自确定性层。

Conclusion: 确定性执行后检查是提升可靠性的关键，但效果方向明确而幅度不确定；需更多组件消融和测试。

中文摘要：LLM分析代理在SQL语法准确性上被评估，但生产环境中的失败形态不同：存在两个有效业务定义的问题、仓库无法回答的问题、模式更改后的废弃列，以及执行成功但返回错误业务数字的查询。没有任何执行匹配指标能够对它们进行评分。本文介绍了WarehouseReliabilityBench，一个包含400个冻结任务、基于两个合成仓库的基准，其中大约一半的正确响应是澄清、弃权或拒绝，配有固定的分母，并在数字存在之前预注册了配对自助法以固定每个声称动词。QueryProof，一个7B代理，使用从语义层和物理目录中推导出的规则来确定其行为，并对每个答案通过确定性的执行后检查进行门控。在一次性评估的80任务合成测试分割上，QueryProof相对于直接提示的32B基线，在商业真相率上提升了+0.237 [+0.112, +0.375]，同时每个正确答案的成本降低了71.0%；与成本匹配的少样本基线相比，准确性增益保持，但成本差异未解决。这比较的是系统而非模型规模：32B基线没有获得任何脚手架。错误成功从返回答案的0.754下降到0.351，并且在可回答的任务上没有返回错误数字（0/24），尽管有13个答案指向需要澄清或弃权的问题。移除路由层变化不大（0.562对0.537），因此结果不依赖于升级。在验证集上调整的路由在测试集上过度弃权，拟合的置信度模型输给了它所替代的启发式方法。重采样模板族而非任务，使两个准确率区间都扩大到包含零，因此效应的方向比其幅度更受支持。增益跟踪确定性层，但未进行组件消融。

Original abstract: LLM analytics agents are evaluated on SQL syntax accuracy, but production failures look different: questions with two valid business definitions, questions the warehouse cannot answer, deprecated columns after a schema change, and queries that execute successfully while returning the wrong business number. No execution-match metric can score them. This paper introduces WarehouseReliabilityBench, 400 frozen tasks over two synthetic warehouses in which roughly half the correct responses are a clarification, an abstention or a refusal, with pinned denominators and a pre-registered paired bootstrap fixing each claim verb before the numbers existed. QueryProof, a 7B agent, uses rules derived from a semantic layer and physical catalog to determine its behaviour, and gates every answer on deterministic post-execution checks. On an 80-task synthetic test split evaluated once, QueryProof outperforms a direct-prompted 32B baseline by +0.237 [+0.112, +0.375] Business Truth Rate at 71.0% lower cost per correct answer; against a cost-matched few-shot baseline the accuracy gain holds but the cost difference does not resolve. This compares systems rather than model sizes: the 32B baseline receives none of the scaffolding. False success falls from 0.754 to 0.351 of returned answers, and no wrong number was returned on an answerable task (0 of 24), though 13 answers went to questions requiring clarification or abstention. Removing the routing layer changes little (0.562 against 0.537), so the result does not depend on escalation. Routing tuned on validation over-abstains on test, and the fitted confidence model loses to the heuristic it replaced. Resampling template families rather than tasks widens both accuracy intervals to include zero, so the effect's direction is better supported than its magnitude. The gain tracks the deterministic layer, though no component ablation was run.

</details>


### [81] [P$^{3}$: Joint Program-and-Proof Planning for Verified Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09277)
*Zenan Li,Ziran Yang,Peiyang Song,Zhaoyu Li,Kaiyu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 联合规划程序与证明的编码 agent，显著提升验证代码生成成功率和效率。

TL;DR: 提出P^3，一个LLM智能体工作流，在验证性代码生成中先联合规划程序与证明再实现，并引入Lean4Commit0基准；在多个基准和模型上取得最高求解率，提升4.6-11.2个百分点，同时降低约40%API成本和37%时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证性代码生成的主流流程先合成程序再证明其正确性，这种串行解耦在实践低效且无效：未考虑证明的程序可能微妙错误或结构难验证，导致LLM陷入代码-证明交替修补的脆弱循环。

Method: 受Dijkstra启发，提出P^3：基于LLM的智能体工作流，先从规范中推导统一的程序与证明计划，再在此共享计划下细化实现与证明脚手架；同时构建Lean4Commit0基准，从真实软件仓库提取核心API并翻译其需求为Lean任务，用于现实场景评估。

Result: 在四个前沿LLM后端上，P^3在Verina、AlgoVeri和Lean4Commit0的每个基准-模型设置中均取得最高求解率；相比更强基线，困难子集上求解率提高4.6-11.2个百分点，每任务API成本降低最多约40%，墙钟时间降低最多约37%；消融显示联合规划比仅实现规划提高3.3-8.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 程序与证明应当联合规划而非顺序执行；P^3通过统一计划显著提升验证性代码生成的效果和效率，Lean4Commit0为现实库级验证提供了评测基础。

中文摘要：验证性代码生成要求大语言模型（LLM）同时生成可执行程序以及该程序满足形式化规范的可机检证明，从而有望构建出“构造即正确”的软件。实际中的主流工作流将问题的两半解耦：先综合程序，再尝试证明其正确性。我们观察到，这种串行流水线在实践中既可能低效也可能无效。一个未预判其证明而生成的程序可能含有微妙的错误或在结构上难以验证，迫使LLM陷入脆弱的修复循环，在修补代码和修补证明之间交替进行。受Dijkstra关于程序及其正确性论证应携手开发的观点的启发，我们提出P^3，一种基于LLM的智能体工作流，它首先从规范中推导出统一的程序与证明计划，然后在此共享计划下细化实现和证明脚手架。为了在现实场景中评估验证性代码生成，我们进一步引入Lean4Commit0，一个从真实软件仓库提取核心API并将其需求（包括跨API的关系规范）翻译为Lean任务、基于仓库的库级基准。使用四个前沿LLM后端，我们在Verina、AlgoVeri以及我们的Lean4Commit0基准上评估P^3，它在每个基准-模型设置中都取得了最高的求解率。与更强的基线相比，它在每个基准的困难子集上将求解率提高了4.6至11.2个百分点，并将每任务API成本最多降低约40%，墙钟时间最多降低约37%。一项针对性的消融研究进一步显示，相对于仅实现规划，联合规划程序与证明带来了3.3至8.3个百分点的增益，从而分离出联合规划的好处。

Original abstract: Verified code generation asks a large language model (LLM) to generate both an executable program and a machine-checkable proof that the program meets a formal specification, promising software that is correct by construction. The de facto workflow decouples the two halves of the problem: first synthesize a program, then attempt to prove it correct. We observe that this sequential pipeline can be both ineffective and inefficient in practice. A program generated without anticipating its proof can be subtly incorrect or structurally difficult to verify, forcing the LLM into brittle repair loops that alternate between patching the code and patching the proof. Inspired by Dijkstra's view that a program and its correctness argument should be developed hand in hand, we propose $P^3$, an LLM-based agentic workflow that first derives a unified program-and-proof plan from the specification, then elaborates the implementation and proof scaffold under this shared plan. To evaluate verified code generation in realistic settings, we further introduce Lean4Commit0, a repository-derived, library-level benchmark built by extracting core APIs from real-world software repositories and translating their requirements, including relational specifications across APIs, into Lean tasks. Using four frontier LLM backends, we evaluate $P^3$ on Verina, AlgoVeri, and our Lean4Commit0 benchmark, where it achieves the highest solve rate in every benchmark--model setting. Compared with the stronger baseline, it improves solve rates by 4.6--11.2 percentage points and reduces per-task API cost by up to roughly 40\% and wall-clock time by up to roughly 37\% on the difficult subset of each benchmark. A targeted ablation further shows gains of 3.3--8.3 points over implementation-only planning, isolating the benefit of planning the program and proof jointly.

</details>


### [82] [ComboShoppingBench: Evaluating LLM Agents for Budget-Constrained Basket Shopping with Coupons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09282)
*Adrian Li,Kelong Mao,Yudong Guo,Heming Xia,Xinwei Yang,Lirui Luo,Jace Wong,Pu Yao,Sulong Xu,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 预算约束购物 agent 评测基准，含确定性校验，对一般 agent 评测方法有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出ComboShoppingBench，一个结合LLM语义评判与确定性约束验证的组合购物智能体基准，实验表明现有强智能体仍面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物评估要么使用精确匹配指标，但多种组合均可满足同一请求；要么仅依赖语义评估，无法捕获不可行订单、无效优惠券组合或错误支付等问题，因此需要可验证的组合购物基准。

Method: 构建模拟商业和外卖环境，通过探索智能体生成可行且语义连贯的见证组合，并据此生成优惠券、预算约束、用户查询和对齐的评估标准；评估时使用LLM评判语义满足度与回答忠实性，同时用确定性验证检查商品ID、预算合规性和优惠券最优性。

Result: 在多种LLM智能体上的实验显示，即使强智能体在ComboShoppingBench上表现挣扎，准确性和约束满足能力不足，说明该基准能有效暴露现有智能体的弱点。

Conclusion: 可靠、受约束感知的组合购物仍是开放挑战，ComboShoppingBench为评估和提升智能体在该任务上的能力提供了重要测试平台和改进方向。

中文摘要：现实世界中的购物通常需要构建一组互补商品，而非检索单个产品。这类组合购物任务出现在设备安装、备餐、活动策划和团体外卖点单等场景中，需要对商品兼容性、可用性、店铺级要求、配送费、优惠券和预算进行联合推理。评估具有挑战性，因为多个商品组合可能满足同一请求，使得精确匹配指标不适用，而仅靠语义评估无法检测不可行的订单、无效的优惠券组合或错误的支付。我们提出了ComboShoppingBench，一个在模拟商业和外卖环境中进行开放式但可验证的商品组合构建的智能体购物基准。在任务合成阶段，一个探索智能体构建一个可行且语义连贯的可购买商品组合；该见证（witness）指导优惠券、预算约束、用户查询和对齐的评估标准的生成。在评估阶段，LLM评判者评估语义满意度、回答质量和主张忠实性，同时确定性验证检查商品ID有效性、预算合规性和优惠券最优性。使用多种LLM智能体的实验表明，即使强智能体在ComboShoppingBench上也表现困难，突显了在可靠、受约束感知的组合购物方面仍有很大的改进空间。

Original abstract: Real-world shopping often requires constructing a basket of complementary items rather than retrieving a single product. Such combo-shopping tasks arise in device setup, meal preparation, event planning, and group takeout ordering, requiring joint reasoning about item compatibility, availability, store-level requirements, delivery fees, coupons, and budgets. Evaluation is challenging because multiple baskets may satisfy the same request, making exact-match metrics unsuitable, whereas semantic evaluation alone cannot detect infeasible orders, invalid coupon combinations, or incorrect payments. We introduce ComboShoppingBench, an agentic shopping benchmark for open-ended yet verifiable basket construction in a simulated commerce and takeout environment. During task synthesis, an exploration agent constructs a feasible and semantically coherent basket of purchasable products; this witness guides the generation of coupons, budget constraints, user queries, and aligned evaluation rubrics. During evaluation, LLM judges assess semantic satisfaction, response quality, and claim faithfulness, while deterministic validation checks product-ID validity, budget compliance, and coupon optimality. Experiments with diverse LLM agents demonstrate that even strong agents struggle on ComboShoppingBench, highlighting substantial room for improvement in reliable, constraint-aware combo shopping.

</details>


### [83] [CoRE: Consensus Rewards via Equilibrium for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09324)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 用均衡动力学替代多数投票构造自监督测试时奖励，是通用LLM测试时RL/自我改进方法，可迁移到agent优化。

TL;DR: 提出CoRE，用图上的复制动态替代多数投票，为测试时强化学习提供更精细的共识奖励，在多个基准上提升性能并加快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票机制会丢弃正确的少数答案，并对所有匹配多数的采样给予相同分数，导致奖励信号粗糙，无法有效区分不同质量的采样；需要一种更精细的共识机制来利用采样间的结构信息。

Method: 将N个采样构建成图，边综合答案一致性、推理相似性和生成置信度；利用复制动态提取主导集，得到精细伪标签、逐采样分级奖励和逐问题一致性门控。CoRE以多数投票为特例，通过块值分析给出恢复正确少数的阈值，并利用置信度校准降低该阈值。

Result: 在7个骨干网络和5个基准（42个模型-基准组合，3个种子）上，CoRE相比未训练基线平均提升+21.7分，优于多数投票TTRL的+20.4分；在可争议一致性的场景中最高超过投票基线+7.5分，并以少54%-70%的步数达到投票基线的平台精度。

Conclusion: 共识而非计数：将采样组视为图而非投票箱，将脆弱的投票转换为经过校准的、分级的自监督奖励，且不增加额外采样成本。

中文摘要：在未标注的测试数据上，强化学习缺乏真实的奖励信号；测试时强化学习方法从模型自身的采样中推导出奖励，奖励那些与N个采样答案的多数投票相匹配的采样。这种投票在正确答案属于少数时会将其丢弃，并且对所有与多数匹配的采样给出相同的分数。我们将其替换为CoRE（基于均衡的一致性奖励）：N个采样构成一个图，其边结合了答案一致性、推理相似性和生成置信度，复制动态提取其主导集，从而产生精细的伪标签、逐采样的分级奖励以及逐问题的一致性门控。CoRE严格推广了投票：多数投票是其特例；块值分析给出了一个清晰的阈值，表明一致性何时能在更大的错误多数中恢复正确的少数；置信度校准被证明能以乘法方式降低该阈值。在七个骨干网络和五个基准测试（42个模型-基准组合，每个三个种子）上，CoRE相比未训练基线平均提升+21.7分，而多数投票TTRL为+20.4分；在一致性存在争议的所有地方获胜，相对于投票的优势最高达+7.5分，并以比投票基线少54%-70%的步数达到其平台精度。共识而非计数：将采样组视为图而不是投票箱，将脆弱的投票转变为经过校准的分级的自监督奖励，且不增加额外采样成本。

Original abstract: On unlabeled test data, reinforcement learning lacks a ground-truth reward; test-time RL methods derive one from the model's own roll-outs, rewarding those that match the majority vote over $N$ sampled answers.
