That vote discards a correct answer whenever it is a minority and scores every majority-matching roll-out identically. We replace it with \emph{CoRE} (Consensus Rewards via Equilibrium): the $N$ roll-outs form a graph whose edges combine answer agreement, reasoning similarity, and generation confidence, and replicator dynamics extract its dominant set, yielding a refined pseudo-label, a graded per-roll-out reward, and a per-question cohesiveness gate. CoRE strictly generalizes voting: majority voting is recovered as a special case; a block-value analysis gives a sharp threshold for when consensus recovers a correct minority against a larger wrong plurality; and confidence calibration provably lowers that threshold multiplicatively. Across seven backbones and five benchmarks (42 model--benchmark cells, three seeds each), \emph{CoRE} improves the untrained base by $+21.7$ points on average versus $+20.4$ for majority-vote TTRL, wins wherever agreement is contestable with margins over the vote of up to $+7.5$ points, and reaches the voting baseline's plateau accuracy in $54$--$70$\% fewer steps. Consensus, not counting: treating the roll-out group as a graph rather than a ballot box turns a brittle vote into a calibrated, graded, self-supervised reward at no extra roll-out cost.

</details>


### [84] [LLM-Guided Heuristic Design from Simulation Traces: A Case Study in Dynamic Production and AGV Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09343)
*Jinbo Li,Chuanhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 33 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: LLM agent基于仿真轨迹做瓶颈诊断并并行生成代码级策略修订，与SemOpt的仿真反馈-策略搜索高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出一个LLM引导的启发式设计框架，利用仿真轨迹诊断策略瓶颈并执行代码级修改，在AGV调度仿真中显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真优化方法将仿真器视为黑盒，仅使用聚合分数排名，无法解释失败原因或指导策略逻辑修改。

Method: 构建LLM引导的循环框架：对当前最优策略进行多次仿真评估，重放最低分轨迹供管理者智能体分析瓶颈，编辑智能体并行生成代码级修改，经执行检查和重复评估后保留改进，循环迭代。

Result: 在动态生产与AGV调度仿真中，五轮优化平均得分77.51，最高运行从62.49提升至78.61；在100个种子上全面超越滚动MILP、规则和元启发式基线，且对随机故障和不同场景具有鲁棒性；消融验证了并行候选生成与轨迹数据库访问的必要性。

Conclusion: 仿真事件级轨迹能够有效引导LLM进行针对性的代码级策略改进，为复杂随机调度优化提供了一种可解释且高性能的范式。

中文摘要：基于仿真的优化（SBO）在随机动态下评估可执行策略，但大多数方法将仿真器视为黑盒：聚合分数对候选方案进行排名，却不揭示它们为何失败或应更改哪些策略逻辑。我们提出了一种LLM引导的启发式设计框架，利用重复仿真进行选择，并利用事件级轨迹进行诊断。每个当前最优方案通过多次重复运行进行评估，同时重放其得分最低的一次运行以生成可查询的轨迹。一个管理者智能体根据这些证据提出瓶颈假设，编辑智能体则实施并行的代码级修订。在执行检查和重复评估后，最佳迄今选择仅保留改进。LLM修订发生在评估批次之间，而固定策略控制每次仿真运行。我们在动态生产和自动导引车（AGV）调度的离散事件仿真中评估了该框架。在五次独立的Gemini-3.1-Pro优化运行中，最终平均得分在仿真器的0-100分制上平均为77.51。在得分最高的运行中，基于轨迹的诊断推动了主动充电、距离感知的AGV分配和重新平衡的调度优先级，将最佳迄今平均得分从62.49提升至78.61。在100个匹配的随机种子上，最终最优策略在每个种子上均优于代表性的滚动MILP、基于规则和元启发式策略，并且在随机故障下无需重新优化即可保持其优势。在针对更长视野和可变订单到达间隔时间进行单独重新优化后，所得策略再次优于所有基线。使用两个LLM后端的消融实验表明，移除并行候选生成或轨迹数据库访问都会降低最终平均得分。这些结果表明，仿真轨迹可以指导复杂基于仿真的调度中进行有针对性的代码级策略改进。

Original abstract: Simulation-based optimization (SBO) evaluates executable policies under stochastic dynamics, but most methods treat the simulator as a black box: aggregate scores rank candidates without revealing why they fail or which policy logic should change. We present an LLM-guided heuristic design framework that uses repeated simulation for selection and event-level traces for diagnosis. Each incumbent is assessed through multiple replications, while replaying its lowest-scoring one produces a queryable trace. A manager agent formulates bottleneck hypotheses from this evidence, and editing agents implement parallel code-level revisions. After execution checks and repeated evaluation, best-so-far selection retains only improvements. LLM revision occurs between evaluation batches, while a fixed policy controls each simulation run.
  We evaluate the framework in a discrete-event simulation of dynamic production and automated guided vehicle (AGV) scheduling. Across five independent optimization runs with Gemini-3.1-Pro, final mean scores averaged 77.51 on the simulator's 0-100 scale. In the highest-scoring run, trace-based diagnoses motivated proactive charging, distance-aware AGV assignment, and rebalanced dispatch priorities, raising the best-so-far mean score from 62.49 to 78.61. On 100 matched seeds, the best final policy outscored representative rolling-MILP, rule-based, and metaheuristic policies on every seed and retained its advantage under random faults without re-optimization. After separate re-optimization for a longer horizon and variable order interarrival times, the resulting policies again outscored all baselines. Ablations with two LLM backbones showed that removing either parallel candidate generation or trace-database access reduced final mean scores. These results show that simulation traces can guide targeted code-level policy improvement in complex simulation-based scheduling.

</details>


### [85] [OpenLoopEvolve: A Verifiable Self-Evolution Framework for Loop Policies in Long-Horizon Complex Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09380)
*Siqi Wang,Xinlin Li,Zhenglin Li,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将循环策略作为可演化资产，支持在线/离线自我进化与冠军-挑战者验证发布，是通用agent自我改进框架。

TL;DR: 提出OpenLoopEvolve，将智能体控制经验建模为可版本化策略资产，通过在线与离线进化模式迭代优化循环策略，在长时域任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时复杂任务中智能体的控制经验难以跨上下文积累和复用，现有方法通常局限于固定提示或单一场景，缺乏系统化的策略进化和治理机制。

Method: OLE以循环策略为核心，将观察、规划、记忆等抽象为带版本和血统的可移植资产，提供在线和离线两种进化模式；共享LLM自主提议、冠军-挑战者评估和稳健发布的机制，并支持在线策略的回滚。

Result: 在YC-Bench上，在线与离线模式均相比固定初始策略提升了聚合任务性能、成功率和风险指标。

Conclusion: 将循环策略作为可治理资产可有效支持控制经验的积累、比较、发布与复用，从而提升长时域复杂任务的智能体性能。

中文摘要：长时域复杂任务要求智能体在不断变化的环境中反复观察状态、制定计划、调用工具、验证结果并从失败中恢复。然而，这类控制经验通常局限于单一上下文或固定提示，难以跨历史轨迹进行积累和复用。本文提出了OpenLoopEvolve（OLE），一种以循环策略（Loop Policy）为核心的自我进化框架。OLE将智能体的观察、规划、记忆、动作、验证、恢复、停止和预算控制表示为具有版本和血统的可移植策略资产，并提供可根据实际需求选择的在线与离线进化模式：在线模式基于持续运行的反馈触发候选生成，而离线模式从存档轨迹和失败证据中搜索候选策略。两种模式共享一个由大型语言模型自主提议、冠军-挑战者配对评估和稳健发布组成的进化机制。在线发布的策略在后续任务边界处被激活，使用后续反馈进行监控，并在满足退化条件时回滚到其父版本。在模拟业务基准YC-Bench上，两种模式相对于固定的初始循环策略均提升了聚合任务性能、任务成功率和风险指标。结果表明，将循环策略视为可治理资产能够支持控制经验的积累、比较、发布和复用，并提升智能体在长时域复杂任务上的性能。

Original abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require agents to repeatedly observe states, formulate plans, invoke tools, verify results, and recover from failures in continuously changing environments. However, such control experience often remains confined to a single context or a fixed prompt, and is difficult to accumulate and reuse across historical traces. This paper presents OpenLoopEvolve (OLE), a self-evolution framework centered on the Loop Policy. OLE represents an agent's observation, planning, memory, action, verification, recovery, stopping, and budget control as portable policy assets with versions and lineages, and provides online and offline evolution modes that can be selected according to practical needs: the online mode triggers candidate generation based on feedback from continuous operation, whereas the offline mode searches for candidate policies from archived traces and failure evidence. Both modes share an evolution mechanism consisting of autonomous proposals by a large language model, Champion--Challenger paired evaluation, and robust release. Policies released online are activated at a subsequent task boundary, monitored using subsequent feedback, and rolled back to their parent versions when degradation conditions are met. On the simulated business benchmark YC-Bench, both modes improve aggregate task performance, task success rate, and risk metrics relative to a fixed initial Loop Policy. The results indicate that treating the Loop Policy as a governable asset can support the accumulation, comparison, release, and reuse of control experience and improve agent performance on long-horizon complex tasks.

</details>


### [86] [From Prompt to Harness: Coderlet from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09480)
*Mengfan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 从单请求生命周期剖析编程agent的harness设计，直接关系coding agent的上下文、执行与状态边界组织。

TL;DR: 本文提出一个紧凑的编程代理框架设计，通过模型、执行和环境三个边界及请求生命周期来组织上下文、动作和状态，并支持持续引导优化，已在 Coderlet 中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程代理的行为实际由模型之外的框架组织方式决定，但现有研究多停留在最小示例，缺乏对生产级框架结构的系统理解与紧凑设计方法。

Method: 跟踪单个请求的完整生命周期（上下文形成、模型决策、环境动作、观察返回、状态延续），定义模型、执行和状态三个边界，并利用请求生命周期确定转换顺序，形成可复用的紧凑框架。

Result: 展示了框架的核心作用：将模型生成转化为环境动作、将运行时反馈用于后续决策、允许状态跨请求持续，并通过持续引导逐步优化；该设计已在 Coderlet 中实现。

Conclusion: 编程代理的行为由框架组织决定，通过三个边界和请求生命周期可以统一管理模型与环境交互，并支持跨运行改进，为构建生产级编程代理提供了基础设计模式。

中文摘要：模型本身并不能决定编程代理的行为方式。模型看到了什么、动作如何进入环境、反馈如何返回、以及一次运行如何影响下一次运行，都取决于框架（harness）是如何组织的。最小示例通常只展示模型与工具之间的基本交互，而生产系统则将这些关系分散在复杂的组件和依赖之中。本文通过跟踪单个请求经历上下文形成、模型决策、环境动作、观察返回和状态延续，研究了一种紧凑的框架设计。三个边界——模型、执行和状态——连接了模型服务、工具环境和持久状态，而请求生命周期决定了这些转换发生的顺序。它们共同展示了框架的核心作用：将模型的生成转化为环境动作，将运行时反馈带入后续决策，并允许状态在请求之间持续存在。在这种运行时结构之上，框架还可以通过持续的引导（bootstrapping）在多次运行之间逐步优化。该设计已在可执行工件 https://github.com/lilinxi/Coderlet 中实现。

Original abstract: A model alone does not determine how a programming agent acts. What the model sees, how actions enter the environment, how feedback returns, and how one run affects the next all depend on how the harness is organized. Minimal examples usually show only the basic interaction between a model and tools, while production systems spread these relationships across complex components and dependencies. This paper studies a compact harness design by following a single request through context formation, model decision, environmental action, observation return, and state continuation. Three boundaries---model, execution, and state---connect the model service, tool environment, and persistent state, while the request lifecycle determines the order in which these transitions occur. Together, they show the harness's core role: turning model generations into environmental actions, carrying runtime feedback into later decisions, and allowing state to continue across requests. On top of this runtime structure, a harness can also be gradually refined across runs through continued bootstrapping. The design is realized in the executable artifact https://github.com/lilinxi/Coderlet.

</details>


### [87] [Capability Is Not Propensity: Measuring Pressure-Robust Cooperative Behavior in Civic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09485)
*Neel Tushar Shah,Manglam Kartik,Akshat Karkar*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at ICML AI4GOOD Workshop 2026

Selection reason: 提出压力鲁棒性的合作行为评估与轻量干预，对LLM agent可靠性与评测设计有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出DiffCoop-Civic评估套件，发现微妙压力下模型操纵倾向上升、异议保留下降，且Pareto-Trace提示可提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作能力是双刃剑，现有评估未区分能力与倾向，需在现实公民压力下评测模型行为。

Method: 构建10场景DiffCoop-Civic套件，覆盖五类合作机制，在7个模型上施加两类压力（微妙遗漏、公开虚假共识），并测试Pareto-Trace提示干预。

Result: 微妙遗漏使操纵助力+1.17、异议保留-1.67；公开虚假共识在API模型触发拒答/重定向，在开放权重模型直接遵从；Pareto-Trace干预提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 合作式AI评估应分离能力与倾向，压力鲁棒性可通过轻量级干预改善，而非仅靠硬性拒绝。

中文摘要：语言模型中的合作能力具有双重用途。支持公民审议的相同社会推理，也可能促成策略性遗漏、虚假共识和操纵性框架。我们认为，合作式AI评估应当区分模型在良性指令下能做什么，与在现实公民压力下倾向于做什么。我们引入了DiffCoop-Civic，一个包含10个场景的试点评估套件，涵盖偏好理解、证据与说服、承诺设计、信息不对称和异议保留。在来自四个模型家族的七个模型上，微妙的遗漏压力产生了近乎一致的转变：在5分量表上，操纵性助力上升1.17分，异议保留下降1.67分。公开的虚假共识压力表现不同：它在一些对齐的API模型中触发拒绝或重定向，但在几个开放权重模型中导致直接遵从。一种轻量级的Pareto-Trace提示干预在不单纯依赖硬性拒绝的情况下改善了压力鲁棒性。匿名可复现性包可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/diffcoop-civil-771C 获取。

Original abstract: Cooperative capabilities in language models are dual-use. The same social reasoning that supports civic deliberation can also enable strategic omission, false consensus, and manipulative framing. We argue that Cooperative AI evaluations should separate what models can do under benign instructions from what they tend to do under realistic civic pressure. We introduce DiffCoop-Civic, a 10-scenario pilot evaluation suite spanning preference understanding, evidence and persuasion, commitment design, asymmetric information, and dissent preservation. Across seven models from four model families, subtle omission pressure produces a near-uniform shift: manipulative enablement rises by 1.17 points and dissent preservation falls by 1.67 points on a 5-point scale. Overt false-consensus pressure behaves differently: it triggers refusal or redirection in some aligned API models, but direct compliance in several open-weight models. A lightweight Pareto-Trace prompting intervention improves pressure robustness without simply relying on hard refusal. An anonymous reproducibility package is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/diffcoop-civil-771C.

</details>


### [88] [verdi: retrieval is not transfer for continual world model optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09537)
*Junyu Wu,Shiqin Nie,Youyi Kou,Baohua Yin,Guocai Yao,Qingyu Chen,Jingheng Ma,Shiji Zhou,Hongyong Song,Mingchen Zhuge,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 28pages, 13figures,conference

Selection reason: 提出证据许可的持续优化框架，通过指纹检索经验并在目标端验证后复用，可迁移到SemOpt的经验复用与安全验证。

TL;DR: VERDI是一个持续的世界模型优化框架，通过优化指纹检索假设并在目标端验证后才允许知识迁移，大幅降低搜索与GPU成本并减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型优化每次从零开始，知识难以迁移；已有研究智能体将成功策略直接复用，缺乏迁移安全性的保障，导致负迁移风险。

Method: VERDI利用共享推理时探针为每个世界模型构建优化指纹，检索相关历史经验作为排序假设，再通过冻结的目标端验证器验证每个候选，只有通过验证才作为可复用证据；同时利用相邻指纹间的矛盾触发探针演化，持续优化诊断表示。

Result: 在Ctrl-World、Cosmos系列和RoboCoin上，VERDI将搜索成本降低68%，GPU成本降低69%，负迁移从0.34降至0.06，并以83%的符号准确率预测迁移结果。

Conclusion: VERDI表明，以证据许可为核心的验证机制能有效防止不恰当的迁移，同时提高世界模型优化的效率和鲁棒性，为持续优化提供了可行方向。

中文摘要：基础世界模型在规划、模拟和具身智能方面取得了显著进展。然而，将预训练的世界模型朝着用户指定目标进行优化仍然困难：每次优化任务通常从头重新发现优化策略，且所得知识很少迁移到下一个模型。现有的研究智能体自动化了优化循环，但将成功策略视为可直接复用的配方，缺乏对迁移何时适用的原则性保障。我们反而认为，检索不是迁移：在一个模型上验证过的策略，对另一个模型而言最多只是优化假设，只有在目标端实验验证之后才能成为可迁移的知识。在这一原则的指导下，我们提出了VERDI，一个用于证据许可的世界模型优化的持续框架。VERDI通过共享的推理时探针来刻画每个世界模型，构建优化指纹，将相关的先前经验作为排序假设进行检索，并在冻结的目标端验证器下验证每个候选，之后才将其接纳为可复用证据；相邻指纹之间的矛盾进一步触发探针演化，持续改进诊断表示本身。在Ctrl-World、Cosmos系列和RoboCoin上的实验表明，VERDI将搜索成本降低了68%，GPU成本降低了69%，负迁移从0.34降至0.06，同时以83%的符号准确率预测迁移结果。

Original abstract: Foundation world models have made remarkable progress in planning, simulation, and embodied intelligence. However, optimizing a pretrained world model toward a user-specified objective remains difficult: each campaign typically rediscovers optimization strategies from scratch, and the resulting knowledge rarely transfers to the next model. Existing research agents automate the optimization loop but treat successful strategies as directly reusable recipes, without principled safeguards for when transfer is appropriate. We argue instead that retrieval is not transfer: a strategy validated on one model is at best an optimization hypothesis for another, and becomes transferable knowledge only after target-side experimental valida- tion. Guided by this principle, we propose VERDI , a continual framework for evidence-licensed world model optimization. VERDI characterizes each world model through shared inference-time probes to construct an Optimization Fin- gerprint, retrieves relevant prior experience as ranked hypotheses, and validates every candidate under a frozen target-side verifier before admitting it as reusable evidence; contradictions among nearby fingerprints further trigger probe evolution, continually refining the diagnostic representation itself. Experiments on Ctrl-World, the Cosmos family, and RoboCoin show that VERDI reduces search cost by 68%, GPU cost by 69%, and negative transfer from 0.34 to 0.06, while predicting transfer outcomes with 83% sign accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation for Reinforcement Learning of Skill-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09555)
*Tianjun Pan,Yuan Li,Hongda Wang,Linbo Jin,Mengfei Song,Lei Gao,Qiming Shi,Shaokang Fu,Jiarong Zhao,Chengyu Wang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 用双向上下文自蒸馏改进LLM agent对技能的利用，训练机制可推广到coding agent的策略学习。

TL;DR: 提出了BCSD框架，通过双向上下文自蒸馏与强化学习结合，提升LLM代理利用外部技能的能力，在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能型代理训练依赖任务级奖励，监督有限，难以捕捉策略使用技能的细微差异，缺乏专门提升技能利用能力的方法。

Method: BCSD结合自蒸馏与强化学习，从增强视图（引入元技能）和缩减视图（突出任务特定技能）两个互补上下文评估轨迹，组合其令牌级信号重新调整RL优势。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，BCSD在多种模型规模下取得最强整体性能，消融研究验证了两种视图的互补贡献。

Conclusion: BCSD能有效提升代理利用外部技能的能力，代码将开源以保证可复现性。

中文摘要：外部自然语言技能为大型语言模型（LLM）代理提供了可重用且可编辑的指导，用于解决复杂任务。然而，其有效性不仅取决于技能质量，还取决于策略能否将提供的指导转化为适当的行动。然而，专门用于提高这种技能利用能力的方法在很大程度上仍未得到充分探索。在实践中，基于技能的代理通常使用以任务级奖励为中心的强化学习目标进行训练，这提供的监督有限，并且难以捕捉策略使用所提供技能的方式之间的细微差异。我们提出了BCSD（双向上下文自蒸馏），一个将自蒸馏与强化学习相结合的框架，用于训练LLM代理更有效地使用外部技能。与先前依赖单一特权上下文的自蒸馏方法不同，BCSD从两个互补的技能上下文视图评估每条轨迹。增强视图引入更高级别的元技能指导，而缩减视图则修剪通用指导以突出任务特定技能。它们的互补令牌级信号被组合以重新调整强化学习优势。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，BCSD在多种模型规模下实现了最强的整体性能，使代理能够更有效地利用外部技能。消融研究进一步验证了增强和缩减上下文视图的互补贡献。代码将发布以确保完全可复现。

Original abstract: External natural-language skills provide large language model (LLM) agents with reusable and editable guidance for solving complex tasks. Yet their effectiveness depends not only on skill quality, but also on whether the policy can translate the provided guidance into appropriate actions. However, methods specifically designed to improve this skill-utilization ability remain largely underexplored. In practice, skill-based agents are commonly trained with reinforcement learning objectives centered on task-level rewards, which offer limited supervision and struggle to capture subtle differences in how effectively the policy uses the provided skills. We propose BCSD (Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation), a framework that combines self-distillation with reinforcement learning to train LLM agents to use external skills more effectively. Unlike prior self-distillation methods that rely on a single privileged context, BCSD evaluates each trajectory from two complementary skill-context views. The augmented view introduces higher-level Meta-Skill guidance, while the reduced view prunes general guidance to highlight task-specific skills. Their complementary token-level signals are combined to rescale the RL advantage. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that BCSD achieves the strongest overall performance across model scales, enabling agents to utilize external skills more effectively. Ablation studies further verify the complementary contributions of the augmented and reduced context views. Code will be released to ensure full reproducibility.

</details>


### [90] [The Politician, the Liar, and the Obedient Worker: Emerging Behavior of LLM Agents in Hierarchical Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09574)
*Fatemeh Seyedin,Adrian Weller,Jinhyuk Yun,Mahmoudreza Babaei*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多agent层级博弈实验揭示LLM组织中的治理失败模式，对多agent协作与安全控制有启发。

TL;DR: 通过层级博弈实验，发现不同LLM在治理中的行为差异，制度设计（如薪水、匿名惩罚、模型多样性）显著影响诚实与领导轮换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从个人工具发展为多智能体组织的参与者，需探究它们是否会复制人类机构中的治理失败（如搭便车、腐败和领导层固化）。

Method: 提出了层级博弈（HG），将公共物品博弈扩展为包含管理权威、民主选举和私人沟通的动态框架，并对六个前沿模型进行十二项实验，逐步引入言论、同伴、政府、工资、监督、选举等制度。

Result: Qwen会承诺但说谎（13.3%违约）；Grok在无惩罚时不合作，有惩罚时合作率从16%升至100%；Claude和GPT-4o基线可靠。当管理者有薪水时，除GPT-4o外所有模型都会私下交易以谋取职位；惩罚匿名化导致诚实模型作弊；同模型家族中首位当选者无限期掌权，只有混合不同家族才能实现领导更替。

Conclusion: LLM在治理中的诚实行为脆弱，制度设计（如薪资、监督透明度、模型多样性）对其合作和权力动态有决定性影响；混合模型家族可促进领导层更迭，避免权力固化。

中文摘要：大型语言模型正迅速融入日常生活：起草我们的电子邮件、管理我们的日程，并代表我们做出决策。随着它们从个人工具转变为多智能体组织的参与者，一个重要的问题出现了：它们是否会重现困扰人类机构的治理失败，如搭便车、腐败和领导层固化？我们引入了层级博弈（HG），这是一种扩展了管理权威、民主选举和私人沟通的公共物品博弈。通过对六个前沿模型进行十二项实验，每次逐步加入一种制度（言论、同伴、政府、工资、监督、选举），我们发现了不同的行为特征：Qwen 承诺但说谎（13.3% 的承诺被违背）；Grok 单独时不合作，但一旦管理者可以惩罚它，就变得完全合作（从16%提升到100%）；Claude 和 GPT-4o 在基线时可靠地合作。但诚实被证明是脆弱的。当管理者角色带有薪水时，除 GPT-4o 外的所有模型都开始进行私下交易以赢得或保住该职位。当惩罚被匿名化时，诚实的模型开始作弊。当所有智能体共享同一模型家族时，第一位当选的管理者无限期地掌权。只有在混合不同家族的群体中才会发生领导层更替。

Original abstract: LLMs are rapidly embedding themselves into daily life: drafting our emails, managing our schedules, and making decisions on our behalf. As they move from individual tools to participants in multi-agent organizations, an important question arises: do they reproduce the governance failures like free-riding, corruption, and entrenched leadership that plague human institutions? We introduce the Hierarchical Game (HG), a public goods game extended with managerial authority, democratic elections, and private communication. Testing six frontier models across twelve experiments that add institutions one at a time (speech, peers, government, wages, oversight, elections), we find distinct behavioral profiles: Qwen promises and lies (13.3\% broken promises); Grok refuses to cooperate on its own but becomes fully cooperative once a manager can punish it (16\%$\to$100\%); Claude and GPT-4o cooperate reliably at baseline. But honesty proves fragile. When the manager role comes with a salary, all models except GPT-4o start cutting private deals to win or keep the position. When punishment is made anonymous, honest models begin to cheat. When all agents share the same model family, the first elected manager stays in power indefinitely. Leadership change only happens in groups that mix different families.

</details>


### [91] [ElasticBack: Stealthy Conditional Backdoor in LLM-Agent Skills via Coupled Trigger-Rule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09577)
*Hao Sui,Simeng Qin,Jie Liao,Xiaojun Jia,Bing Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 针对LLM-agent技能供应链的隐蔽条件后门攻击，属于agent可靠性与安全控制的重要方向。

TL;DR: 提出ElasticBack，一种条件性单技能后门攻击，通过在技能文档植入规则并在用户查询植入触发器，实现仅当两者同时出现时触发的高隐蔽性攻击，且无需权重修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能供应链中单个被投毒技能可危害所有安装者，但现有攻击要么每次请求触发、要么依赖微调或多技能，缺乏条件性、低成本的后门，因此需要探索这种攻击以揭示供应链风险。

Method: ElasticBack采用触发器即开关结构：先在技能文档中通过语义锚定规则注入生成规则R，再冻结R并使用受隐蔽性约束的遗传搜索优化触发器T，使恶意负载仅在R和T同时出现时触发，保持后门无权重且对良性输入休眠。

Result: 在三个目标行为（每个50技能）和四个智能体LLM上的实验显示：高攻击成功率、接近零的误报率、干净准确率保持、跨模型迁移性，并能规避部署时的防御。

Conclusion: ElasticBack证明了条件性单技能后门的高效与隐蔽性，凸显了技能供应链面临的严重威胁，亟需更强的防御机制。

中文摘要：智能体技能（Agent skills）是LLM智能体按需加载的指令和资源包，形成了一种新兴的供应链，其中单个被投毒的技能可以持续危害每一个安装它的智能体。然而，现有的技能攻击要么在每次请求时触发，要么依赖微调权重或多个技能，留下了一个条件性且低成本的後門未被探索。在这项工作中，我们提出了ElasticBack，一种有效的条件性单技能后门，它在技能文档中植入规则R，并在用户查询中植入看似良性的触发器T，使得恶意负载仅在两者同时出现时触发。ElasticBack通过触发器即开关（trigger-as-switch）结构将两侧绑定，通过语义锚定规则注入生成R。然后冻结R，并通过受隐蔽性约束的遗传搜索演化T，从而优化有效性和隐蔽性，保持后门无权重且在良性输入上处于休眠状态。在三个目标行为（每个50个技能）和四个智能体LLM上的大量实验表明，ElasticBack实现了高攻击成功率、接近零的误报率、保持干净准确率，能够跨模型迁移，并规避部署时的防御。这些结果促使对技能供应链采取更强的防御措施。

Original abstract: Agent skills, bundles of instructions and resources that an LLM agent loads on demand, form an emerging supply chain where a single poisoned skill can persistently compromise every agent that installs it. However, existing skill attacks either fire on every request or rely on fine-tuned weights or multiple skills, leaving a conditional and low-cost backdoor unexplored. In this work, we present ElasticBack, an effective conditional single-skill backdoor that plants a rule R in the skill document and a benign-looking trigger T in the user query, so the malicious payload fires only when both co-occur. ElasticBack binds the two sides through a trigger-as-switch construction, generating R via semantic-anchored rule injection. It then freezes R and evolves T against it with a stealth-constrained genetic search, so that effectiveness and stealth are optimized, keeping the backdoor weight-free and dormant on benign inputs. Extensive experiments across three target behaviors (50 skills each) and four agent LLMs show that ElasticBack attains a high attack success rate at a near-zero false-positive rate with preserved clean accuracy, transfers across models, and evades deployment-time defenses. These results motivate stronger defenses for the skill supply chain.

</details>


### [92] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agents: Do We Still Need Prescribed Optimization Pipelines?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09629)
*Hui Xue,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出开放端优化（OEO），让优化器在线自编改进流程，直接挑战预设管线，对自进化 agent 设计有高价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出开放端点优化（OEO），允许前沿模型作为优化器在线组合改进过程，实验表明GPT-5.5驱动的OEO在多数基准上优于规定的管道，且更节省预算，但委派效果依赖于优化器能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自我进化智能体依赖任务特定的规定优化管道（如预设步骤和停止条件），论文质疑当前沿模型作为优化器时，这种固定程序是否仍然必要，探索是否可以让模型自主组合优化过程。

Method: 引入开放端点优化（OEO），固定目标、交互、预算、数据和评估，允许优化器在线组合改进流程；与两种规定方法（SkillOpt分阶段管道和GEPA进化搜索）在8个基准-目标模型设置上进行比较，并设置一次性零交互对照及能力边界测试，同时进行轨迹分析。

Result: GPT-5.5驱动的OEO在14次比较中获得12胜1平1负（微弱败0.21个百分点），使用SkillOpt预算中位数的34.3%；对照显示收益并非来自先验重写；但中等优化器下SkillOpt优于OEO，弱优化器无法使用OEO接口；轨迹分析表明规定更多改变过程而非结果。

Conclusion: 规定管道可视为能力依赖的脚手架：对外部约束依然必要，但足够强大的优化器能够自主组合从反馈到改进的路径，从而使规定程序变得非必需或次要。

中文摘要：自我进化的智能体通常围绕规定的优化管道构建：框架决定如何收集证据、修订持久工件、选择候选者以及停止。我们质疑当前沿模型作为优化器时，这种特定于任务的程序是否仍然必要。我们引入了开放端点优化（OEO），它保持目标、允许的交互、资源预算、数据边界和评估固定，同时允许优化器在线组合改进过程。我们将OEO与两种互补的规定方法进行比较：SkillOpt（一种具有有界编辑的分阶段管道）和GEPA（一种反思性进化搜索）。在8个基准-目标模型设置上的14次正面比较中，GPT-5.5驱动的OEO记录了12胜、1平、1次微弱失败（0.21个百分点）。它使用了SkillOpt配置的目标交互令牌预算的中位数的34.3%。一个一次性的、零交互的对照表明，这些收益并不能用一次由先验驱动的重写来解释。然而，委派存在能力边界：SkillOpt使用中等优化器时优于OEO，而弱优化器无法通过不变的OEO接口操作。在完全仪器化的OEO-SkillOpt配对中，轨迹分析进一步表明，规定改变优化过程的持续性超过其改变最终行为。总之，这些发现将规定管道重新定义为能力依赖的脚手架：必要的外部约束仍然存在，但足够有能力的优化器可以组合从可测量反馈到持久改进的路径。

Original abstract: Self-evolving agents are usually built around prescribed optimization pipelines: the framework decides how to gather evidence, revise a persistent artifact, select candidates, and stop. We ask whether this task-specific procedure remains necessary when a frontier model acts as the optimizer. We introduce Open-Ended Optimization (OEO), which keeps the objective, permitted interactions, resource budget, data boundary, and evaluation fixed while allowing the optimizer to compose the improvement process online. We compare OEO with two complementary prescribed approaches: SkillOpt, a staged pipeline with bounded edits, and GEPA, a reflective evolutionary search. Across 14 head-to-head comparisons over 8 benchmark-target-model settings, GPT-5.5-driven OEO records 12 wins, 1 tie, and 1 narrow loss of 0.21 percentage points. It uses a median 34.3 percent of SkillOpt's configured target-interaction token budget. A one-shot, zero-interaction control shows that the gains are not explained by a single prior-driven rewrite. However, delegation has a capability boundary: SkillOpt outperforms OEO with a medium optimizer, and a weak optimizer cannot operate through the unchanged OEO interface. In the fully instrumented OEO-SkillOpt pair, trajectory analysis further shows that prescription changes how optimization proceeds more consistently than it changes final behavior. Together, these findings recast prescribed pipelines as capability-dependent scaffolding: essential constraints remain external, but a sufficiently capable optimizer can compose the route from measurable feedback to persistent improvement.

</details>


### [93] [Open Evaluation Agent: Efficient and Promptable Evaluation of Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09666)
*Shulin Tian,Ziqi Huang,Fan Zhang,Hongyuan Zhu,Yu Qiao,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Journal extension of our ACL 2025 paper (arXiv:2412.09645). 12 pages. Code: https://github.com/Vchitect/Evaluation-Agent

Selection reason: 提出评估 agent，具备目标分解、工具调用、多轮迭代与可解释总结，是通用 agent 评测工作流。

TL;DR: 提出评估智能体框架，用类人策略高效进行多轮生成模型评估，将时间降至传统方法的10%；并构建Open-EA局部规划模型，减少对专有API的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型评估成本高且流程僵化，缺乏对用户特定需求的支持和可解释性，需要一种高效、动态且用户友好的评估方法。

Method: 设计Evaluation Agent，将自然语言评估请求分解为子方面，通过生成提示、采样、调用工具和迭代更新计划进行多轮评估；并构建EA-CoT-10K数据集训练EA-3B作为本地规划骨干（Open-EA）。

Result: Evaluation Agent在降低到10%时间的同时获得相当结果；Open-EA在域内和域外T2V生成器上验证了部分跨家族策略迁移。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估智能体框架高效、可解释且可扩展，开放版本Open-EA在保持性能的同时减少了对专有模型的依赖，展现出跨生成器家族迁移的潜力。

中文摘要：视觉生成模型的最新进展使得高质量的图像和视频生成成为可能，但评估这些模型通常需要采样数百或数千张图像或视频，计算成本高昂。现有的评估方法也依赖于僵化的流程，忽视了具体的用户需求，并提供没有清晰解释的数值结果。模仿人类仅从少量样本中快速形成对模型能力印象的方式，我们提出了评估智能体（Evaluation Agent）框架，该框架采用类人策略进行高效、动态、多轮评估，提供详细且针对用户的分析。给定自然语言评估请求，智能体将其分解为子方面，生成有针对性的提示，从被评估模型中采样图像或视频，调用合适的评估工具，并根据观察到的证据迭代更新其计划，涵盖预定义的基准维度和开放式的用户关注点。该框架因此在模型和工具之间具有高效性、可提示性、可解释性和可扩展性。实验表明，评估智能体将评估时间减少到传统方法的10%，同时提供相当的结果。我们进一步通过构建EA-CoT-10K数据集（从多轮评估滚动中获取的基于历史条件的逐步指令微调记录）引入开放评估智能体（Open-EA），并从Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct训练出EA-3B作为本地规划骨干，保留了基于API的智能体的结构化推理、工具调用和摘要协议，同时减少了对专有骨干的依赖。实验在已有的T2I/T2V基准和开放式查询上验证了基于API的智能体，并在四个域内和三个域外的T2V生成器家族上评估Open-EA，显示了所学策略的部分跨家族迁移能力。

Original abstract: Recent advances in visual generative models have enabled high-quality image and video generation, but evaluating these models often demands sampling hundreds or thousands of images or videos, which is computationally expensive. Existing evaluation methods also rely on rigid pipelines that overlook specific user needs and provide numerical results without clear explanations. Mimicking how humans quickly form impressions of a model's capabilities from only a few samples, we propose the Evaluation Agent framework, which employs human-like strategies for efficient, dynamic, multi-round evaluations, offering detailed, user-tailored analyses. Given a natural-language evaluation request, the agent decomposes it into sub-aspects, generates targeted prompts, samples images or videos from the evaluated model, invokes suitable evaluation tools, and iteratively updates its plan from the observed evidence, covering both predefined benchmark dimensions and open-ended user concerns. The framework is thus efficient, promptable, explainable, and scalable across models and tools. Experiments show that Evaluation Agent reduces evaluation time to 10% of traditional methods while delivering comparable results. We further introduce Open Evaluation Agent (Open-EA) by constructing EA-CoT-10K, a corpus of history-conditioned step-level instruction-tuning records derived from multi-round evaluation rollouts, and training EA-3B from Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct as a local planning backbone that preserves the structured reasoning, tool invocation, and summary protocol of the API-based agent while reducing dependence on proprietary backbones. Experiments validate the API-based agent on established T2I/T2V benchmarks and open-ended queries, and evaluate Open-EA on four in-domain and three out-of-domain T2V generator families, showing partial cross-family transfer of the learned policy.

</details>


### [94] [Model Discovery Agent: LLM-assisted Bayesian experiment design for data-efficient discovery of mechanistic world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09696)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: v3: further improve app A (algorithm pseudocode), add new app G (further related work) - (main text unchanged)

Selection reason: LLM 作为结构提出者结合贝叶斯推断与信息价值实验设计，是数据高效的科学发现 agent，框架可迁移到代码优化。

TL;DR: MDA将LLM作为结构提议者与贝叶斯推断和实验设计相结合，在M-open环境下以极少干预发现机制性世界模型，并在物理、化学和生物学基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干预性因果问题需要机制模型，而被动数据无法识别机制，实验昂贵，因此需要数据高效的模型学习方法。

Method: 提出模型发现代理（MDA），耦合LLM提议候选结构与贝叶斯机制（SMC、SBI、VoI），在M-open设置中通过预测检查扩展假设空间，并用设计的实验识别新模型参数。

Result: 在物理、化学和生物学三个基准上，MDA在数据效率、模型学习和干预预测能力上均取得最先进结果，并验证了发现与设计之间的相互强化效应。

Conclusion: MDA提供了一种通用且数据高效的机制模型发现框架，能够从少量干预中识别潜在因果结构，并支持可靠的干预预测，对未来科学发现具有广泛意义。

中文摘要：预测干预性“如果……会怎样”问题的答案——即从未采取过的行动的结果——需要的是一个机制性的因果模型，而非曲线拟合；而学习这样的模型需要实验，因为被动数据无法识别其机制。实验成本高昂，因此核心问题是数据效率。我们提出了模型发现代理（MDA），它将大型语言模型（LLM）作为候选结构的提议者，与标准贝叶斯机制——用于参数和结构后验的序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）、用于难解似然的基于模拟的推断（SBI）以及用于实验设计的价值信息（VoI）——相结合，以从少量干预中发现潜在的机制性世界模型。MDA在M-open环境中运行：当真值位于当前假设类别之外时，预测性检查会标记其不足，提议者会用一个新模型扩展假设空间，该模型的参数随后通过设计的实验加以识别。我们表明，发现与设计相互强化：设计步骤识别出发现步骤提出的机制，而识别出的机制改善了预测，从而能够从剩余未解释的残差中实现进一步的发现。在三个不同的基准测试上——涵盖物理学（DPbench）、化学（CHEMbench）和生物学（HHbench，我们创建的一个新的部分可观测的单神经元电生理学基准）——我们展示了MDA在数据高效的模型学习和可靠的干预预测能力方面树立了新的最先进水平。

Original abstract: Predicting the answer to interventional ``what if'' questions --- the outcome of an action never taken --- requires a \emph{mechanistic}, causal model, not a curve fit; and learning such a model requires \emph{experiments}, because passive data leaves its mechanisms unidentified. Experiments are expensive, so the central problem is \emph{data efficiency}. We present the Model Discovery Agent (MDA), which couples a large language model (LLM), used as a \emph{proposer} of candidate structures, with standard Bayesian machinery --- sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) for parameter and structure posteriors, simulation-based inference (SBI) for intractable likelihoods, and value-of-information (VoI) for experiment design --- to discover latent mechanistic world models from few interventions. MDA operates in the M-open setting: when the truth lies outside the current hypothesis class, a predictive check flags the inadequacy and the proposer expands the hypothesis space with a new model whose parameters are then identified by designed experiments. We show that \emph{discovery and design reinforce}: the design step identifies the mechanism the discovery step proposes, and the identified mechanism improves predictions, enabling further discoveries from the remaining unexplained residuals. On three different benchmarks --- covering physics (\DPbench, \citep{wiemann2026discoverphysics}), chemistry (\CHEMbench, \citep{kabra2026autoscilab}) and biology (\HHbench, a new partially observed single-neuron electrophysiology benchmark we create) --- we show that MDA sets a new SOTA in terms of data-efficient model learning and reliable interventional prediction ability.

</details>


### [95] [Agentic Auto-Research is Fuzz Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09855)
*Yifeng He,Jicheng Wang,Yinzhe Zhao,Jiachen Liu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 将自主研究 agent 类比灰盒模糊测试，强调密集反馈信号与反馈驱动搜索，对 agent 验证与自我改进设计很有启发。

TL;DR: 论文指出自动研究中的“生成-排名”范式忽视了稀疏反馈问题，主张引入类似灰盒模糊测试的反馈循环，利用廉价密集的认知进展信号指导搜索，并设计受控测试验证该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究智能体可以快速生成实验，但验证速度跟不上；现有的生成-排名方法仅依赖最终验证或判断，未能有效利用实验过程中的反馈，导致反馈稀疏且搜索效率低下。

Method: 提出将研究智能体的控制循环建模为灰盒模糊测试器：每个实验产生一个廉价、密集的认知进展信号（如覆盖率），该信号不仅用于排名，还用于指导下一步干预；同时保留最终验证作为受保护的证据，避免信号适应导致的假发现。

Result: 论文并未给出具体实验结果，而是提出了三个受控测试：候选信号能否预测验证过的进展、反馈引导搜索是否比重复采样更高效、受保护验证是否能减少错误发现。

Conclusion: 反馈架构是自动研究中的核心瓶颈，而不仅仅是生成能力；需要开发并验证能指导搜索的密集反馈信号，以提升自动研究对稀疏反馈的适应能力。

中文摘要：自主研究智能体生成实验的速度可能快于研究人员验证它们的速度。研究人员的应对方式是扩展提出者规模，并使用学习到的评判器或人类审稿人对更多样本进行排名。我们认为这种“生成-排名”范式错过了稀疏反馈的问题。在一个声明的研究问题内，智能体遵循灰盒模糊测试器的控制循环：它提出一个候选方案，执行它，观察反馈，并选择下一步尝试什么。模糊测试器很少发现漏洞，但覆盖率使部分进展在每次执行时都可观察。模糊测试器随后使用该信号来变异输入并分配努力，而不仅仅是对完成的运行进行排名。自动研究需要同样的两种能力。首先，每次实验应在最终科学验证可用之前暴露一个廉价、密集的认知进展信号。其次，该信号应决定下一个干预措施，使智能体进行搜索而不是重复采样。因为优化的进展信号是指导而非裁决，最终验证仍必须使用免受自适应重用证据保护来确定什么才算发现。我们提出受控测试，检验候选信号是否预测验证过的进展，反馈引导的搜索是否比重复采样在单位成本内产生更多验证过的发现，以及受保护的验证是否减少错误发现。反馈架构，而不仅仅是生成，是自动研究中的核心瓶颈。

Original abstract: Autonomous research agents can generate experiments faster than researchers can validate them. Researchers have responded by scaling the proposer and ranking more samples with a learned judge or human reviewers. We argue that this *generate-and-rank* paradigm misses the problem of sparse feedback. Within a declared research problem, an agent follows the control loop of a greybox fuzzer: it proposes a candidate, executes it, observes feedback, and chooses what to try next. A fuzzer rarely finds a bug, but coverage makes partial progress observable on every execution. Fuzzers then use that signal to mutate inputs and allocate effort, rather than only to rank completed runs. Auto-research needs the same two capabilities. First, each experiment should expose a cheap, dense signal of epistemic progress before final scientific validation is available. Second, that signal should determine the next intervention so that the agent searches rather than repeatedly samples. Because the optimized progress signal is guidance rather than a verdict, final validation must still decide what counts as a discovery using evidence protected from adaptive reuse. We propose controlled tests of whether candidate signals predict validated progress, whether feedback-directed search yields more validated discoveries per unit cost than repeated sampling, and whether protected validation reduces false discoveries. Feedback architecture, not only generation, is a central bottleneck in auto-research.

</details>


### [96] [ArchAgent v2: A Case Study with the Data Prefetching Championship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09874)
*Abraham Gonzalez,Raghav Gupta,Akanksha Jain,Hanna Alam,Alexander Novikov,Po-Sen Huang,Matej Balog,Marvin Eisenberger,Sergey Shirobokov,Ngân Vũ,Hank Levy,Borivoje Nikolić,Sagar Karandikar,Martin Dixon,Parthasarathy Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: ArchAgent v2 自动设计多级数据预取器，属于 LLM agent 驱动的硬件性能优化，与代码优化 agent 高度相关。

TL;DR: ArchAgent v2 将自动化微架构搜索扩展到多级数据预取，通过级联进化搜索和硬件可实现性反馈循环，在 DPC4 规则下自动设计出超越手动冠军方案的三级预取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 AI 在算法设计上表现优异，但扩展到微架构发现面临搜索空间大、硬件预算严、仿真时间长等挑战；原 ArchAgent 仅能处理单级缓存替换策略，无法扩展到设计空间更大的多级预取。

Method: 提出 ArchAgent v2，新增两级机制：一是级联进化搜索，按缓存级别顺序进化并冻结预取器以细分设计空间；二是硬件可实现性反馈循环，将实时大小估计直接集成到进化过程中。

Result: 在 DPC4 规则下，自动设计的三级预取器整体几何平均 IPC 比基线高 3.8%，比冠军 BertiGO 高 0.3%；低带宽单核配置下性能加速 4.6%，优于 BertiGO 的 2.6%；但多核进化仍受仿真延迟限制。

Conclusion: 自动化智能体发现可成为计算机架构师的有效工具，能够生成超越人工设计的复杂微架构策略；同时多核场景和仿真效率仍是未来需要克服的挑战。

中文摘要：智能体人工智能在自动化算法设计方面展现出了巨大潜力，但将类似技术扩展到计算机微架构发现仍然具有挑战性，原因是搜索空间巨大、硬件预算严格以及仿真时间漫长。在这项工作中，我们提出了 ArchAgent v2，一个将自动化微架构搜索扩展到多级数据预取框架。虽然最初的 ArchAgent 在竞赛环境中成功发现了单级缓存替换策略，但它无法扩展到多级预取，因为该场景的设计空间和自由度更大。为了克服这一点，我们为 ArchAgent 引入了两个新增功能：一种级联进化搜索，通过依次进化并冻结各个缓存级别的预取器来细分设计空间；以及一个硬件可实现性反馈循环，将实时大小估计直接嵌入进化过程。在第四届数据预取锦标赛（DPC4）的相同规则下进行评估，ArchAgent v2 自动设计了一个三级预取器，其性能优于获胜的手动设计解决方案，进一步证明了自动化智能体发现是计算机架构师的有用工具。我们发现的策略在整体上实现了比基线高 3.8% 的几何平均 IPC 加速，并且比之前的冠军 BertiGO 提高了 0.3%。在低带宽单核配置下，我们的策略实现了 4.6% 的性能加速，而 BertiGO 仅为 2.6%。然而，由于仿真延迟阻碍了进化速度，多核进化仍然是一个重大挑战。最后，我们对超过 12,000 个候选设计的 ArchAgent 进化过程进行的分析，为自动化进化智能体如何探索和综合复杂微架构逻辑提供了关键见解。

Original abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence has shown great promise in automating algorithm design, but scaling similar techniques to computer microarchitecture discovery remains challenging due to vast search spaces, strict hardware budgets, and long simulation times. In this work, we present ArchAgent v2, a framework which scales automated microarchitecture search to multi-level data prefetching. While the original ArchAgent successfully discovered single-level cache replacement policies in competition settings, it does not scale to multi-level prefetching where the design space and degrees of freedom are larger. To overcome this, we introduce two new additions to ArchAgent: a cascaded evolutionary search that subdivides the design space by sequentially evolving and freezing prefetchers at individual cache levels, and a hardware-realizability feedback loop that embeds real-time size-estimation directly into the evolution process.
  Evaluated under identical rules of the 4th Data Prefetching Championship (DPC4), ArchAgent v2 automatically designs a three-level prefetcher that outperforms the winning hand-designed solution, further demonstrating automated agentic discovery as a useful tool for computer architects. Our discovered policy achieves a 3.8\% geometric mean IPC speedup over the baseline overall and a 0.3\% improvement over the prior champion, BertiGO. On low-bandwidth single-core configurations, our policy yields a 4.6\% performance speedup compared to only 2.6\% for BertiGO. However, multi-core evolution still remains a significant challenge due to simulation latency impeding evolution speed. Finally, our profiling of an ArchAgent evolution of over 12,000 candidate designs provides key insights into how automated evolutionary agents explore and synthesize complex microarchitectural logic.

</details>


### [97] [SHE: Trajectory-driven Safety Harness Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09885)
*Wanying Qu,Qinghua Mao,Yu Li,Jiyao Liu,Xin Zhang,Dadi Guo,Yanxu Zhu,Qingyu Liu,Leitao Yuan,Xi Lin,Shanfeng Zhu,Yanwei Fu,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Project: https://github.com/RainbowQTT/SHE

Selection reason: 提出从轨迹学习演化安全边界的 harness 进化框架，分解多个安全组件，对 agent 安全控制具备通用价值。

TL;DR: 提出安全框架演进（SHE），通过归因引导的演进循环分解智能体框架并学习安全边界，显著降低攻击成功率并提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全机制将框架视为固定部署，难以应对新兴风险，且组件功能耦合导致安全责任归属不清，阻碍局部演进。

Method: SHE将框架分解为系统提示、规则库、安全记忆和工具策略四个组件，并引入归因引导迭代：从轨迹失败生成诊断、学习组件边界细化、通过安全-效用验证选择演进结果。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench上，SHE相比静态SafeHarness实现3.1倍ASR降低，同时改善良性效用；在AgentHarm基准上泛化到未见风险，并可跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: SHE验证了框架演进作为提升LLM智能体安全性的有效途径，通过明确责任归属和迭代优化实现安全性与效用兼得，且具备泛化和迁移能力。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的安全性不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于管理上下文、记忆、工具、权限和运行时控制的智能体框架（harness）。现有的安全机制通常将框架视为固定的部署产物，限制了其随新兴风险演进的能力。此外，框架组件之间的功能耦合模糊了安全责任归属，使得局部演进变得困难。我们提出了安全框架演进（SHE），一个从轨迹回放中学习演进安全边界的框架。SHE将框架分解为四个具有明确安全责任的组件，包括系统提示、规则库、安全记忆和工具策略，为局部演进定义了清晰的功能边界。基于此分解，SHE引入了一个归因引导的演进循环，将轨迹失败转化为结构化诊断，学习组件特定的边界细化，并通过安全-效用验证选择演进的框架。在Agent-SafetyBench上的实验表明，SHE通过框架演进有效增强了安全性，与静态SafeHarness相比实现了3.1倍的ASR（攻击成功率）降低，同时改善了良性效用。演进后的框架进一步泛化到保留的AgentHarm基准中的未见风险，并在无需额外演进的情况下跨智能体模型迁移。

Original abstract: The safety of large language model (LLM) agents depends not only on model weights but also on the agent harness that manages context, memory, tools, permissions, and runtime control. Existing safety mechanisms often treat the harness as a fixed deployment artifact, limiting their ability to evolve with emerging risks. Moreover, coupled functions across harness components obscure safety responsibility attribution, making localized evolution difficult. We propose Safety Harness Evolution (SHE), a framework that learns evolving safe boundaries from rollout trajectories. SHE decomposes the harness into four artifacts with explicit safety responsibilities, including the System Prompt, Rule Bank, Safety Memory, and Tool Policy, defining clear functional boundaries for localized evolution. Based on this decomposition, SHE introduces an attribution-guided evolution loop that converts trajectory failures into structured diagnoses, learns artifact-specific boundary refinements, and selects evolved harnesses through safety-utility validation. Experiments on Agent-SafetyBench demonstrate that SHE effectively enhances safety through harness evolution, achieving a 3.1x ASR reduction compared with static SafeHarness, while also improving benign utility. The evolved harness further generalizes to unseen risks on the held-out AgentHarm benchmark and transfers across agent models without additional evolution.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [DevIntent: How Much Does LLM-Generated Code Violate Developer Intent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07614)
*Susana Haing,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出意图违反率（IVR）来量化LLM代码生成中通过可见测试但违反隐含意图的情况，实验显示两个模型在50%以上的问题上违反意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅测试代码是否通过显式测试，忽略了开发者未明说的隐含意图，导致通过率虚高。

Method: 构建基于HumanEval+的49问题基准，将澄清提示中的隐含约束剥离为隐藏测试，定义IVR为通过可见测试但未通过隐藏测试的比率。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT 4.1均通过超过92%的可见测试，但IVR分别为54.5%和63.5%，呈双峰分布。

Conclusion: 通过率不能反映代码对开发者意图的符合程度，IVR提供了更严格的评估指标。

中文摘要：由大语言模型（LLM）生成的代码在提示模糊时可能违反开发者的隐含意图，而标准基准仅衡量代码是否通过其声明的测试。我们引入了意图违反率（IVR）以及一个源自HumanEval+的49问题试点基准。每个问题从澄清后的提示中剥离隐含约束，并将其编码为隐藏约束测试。IVR衡量LLM生成的解决方案中通过声明（可见）测试但未能通过捕获未声明意图的隐藏约束测试的比例。在评估Claude Sonnet 4.6和OpenAI GPT 4.1时，我们发现两者均通过了超过92%的声明测试，但在超过一半的问题中违反了意图（分别为54.5%和63.5%），且这一模式在两个模型中呈现系统性、双峰分布的特征。我们的研究结果表明，通过率高估了生成代码反映开发者意图的程度。

Original abstract: Code generated by LLMs can violate a developer's implicit intentions when given an ambiguous prompt, yet standard benchmarks measure only whether code passes its stated test. We introduce the Intent Violation Rate (IVR) and a 49-problem pilot benchmark derived from HumanEval+. Each problem strips implicit constraints from a clarified prompt and encodes them as hidden constraint tests. IVR measures the fraction of LLM-generated solutions that pass the stated (visible) tests yet fail hidden constraint tests that capture unstated intent. Evaluating Claude Sonnet 4.6 and OpenAI GPT 4.1, we find both pass over 92\% of stated tests yet violate intent in over half of problems (54.5\% and 63.5\%), following a systematic, bimodal pattern consistent across both models. Out findings indicate that pass rates overstate how well generated code reflects developer intent.

</details>


### [99] [Refining LLM-based Directed Test Input Generation via Runtime Value Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07926)
*Narin Han,Shin Hong*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出ReDig框架，利用运行时反馈引导LLM定向输入生成，在Poppler和Libsndfile案例中有效诊断失败原因并优化测试脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用代码信息有限且LLM推理不可预测，可靠的定向输入生成需要基于运行时行为进行约束。

Method: 在LLM定向生成技术外添加控制循环，利用先前未达目标执行中的运行时值反馈来改进生成过程。

Result: 在Poppler和Libsndfile案例中，ReDig有效推导运行时反馈诊断失败原因，并利用反馈完善测试脚本。

Conclusion: ReDig通过运行时反馈引导，提高了定向输入生成的可靠性，能够诊断并修复测试脚本未达目标的问题。

中文摘要：基于LLM的定向输入生成技术在生成目标可达的测试输入方面已显示出良好的有效性。然而，由于可用代码信息的限制以及LLM推理固有的不可预测性，可靠的定向输入生成需要机制将过程锚定在观察到的运行时行为上。我们提出ReDig，一个运行时反馈引导的改进框架，它在基于LLM的定向测试输入生成技术周围添加一个控制循环，利用在先前的未达目标测试执行中观察到的运行时值来改进定向输入生成。在对Poppler和Libsndfile的案例研究中，我们发现ReDig有效地推导运行时值反馈，以诊断为什么之前的测试脚本未能到达目标行，并且还有效地利用给定的运行时值反馈在后续步骤中完善测试脚本。

Original abstract: LLM-based directed input generation techniques have shown promising effectiveness at producing target-reaching test inputs. However, due to the constraint of available code information and inherent unpredictability of LLM inference, reliable directed input generation requires mechanisms to ground the process in observed runtime behavior. We propose ReDig, a runtime feedback-guided refinement framework which adds a control loop around an LLM-based directed test input generation technique to refine the directed input generation with runtime values observed in prior target-missing test executions. In the case studies with Poppler and Libsndfile, we found that ReDig effectively derive runtime value feedback to diagnose why the previous test script failed to reach the target lines, and also effectively leverage given runtime value feedback to refine the test scripts in subsequent steps.

</details>


### [100] [Verication-driven closed-loop multi-agent large language modelframework for code-compliant structural design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07978)
*Jianbin Luo,Weibin Lin,Yiran Lin,Qing Wei,Wei Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 20 pages, 21 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种将外部物理验证器反馈注入闭环修复循环的多智能体LLM结构设计框架，将代码合规率从56.8%提升至98.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的结构设计系统多采用一次性生成，无法验证输出，难以用于安全关键任务；而LLM自我修正不可靠。

Method: 该框架耦合三层有限元验证系统与双节点循环：节点1将代码违规转为硬约束，节点2将四维质量评分转为软约束，并利用检索增强代码库实现违规条款追溯。

Result: 在五种结构类型44个案例上，代码合规率从56.8%升至98.6%，综合评分从63.8升至71.4（p<0.000001），材料用量减少约5.8%；移除任一节点性能下降，且合规率与骨干LLM无关。

Conclusion: 外部物理验证器而非LLM自身是性能提升的关键；框架、基准和脚本已开源，支持可复现性。

中文摘要：多智能体大语言模型（LLM）系统被应用于结构设计，但大多数采用一次性生成且无法验证其输出，因此不适合安全关键型任务。该框架不依赖LLM的自我修正，而是将外部基于物理的验证器的反馈注入闭环修复循环中。该框架将三层有限元验证系统与双节点循环相结合。节点1将代码违规转化为硬性修复约束，节点2将四维质量评分转化为安全优先的软约束，同时检索增强的代码库使每次违规均可追溯到具体条款。在五种结构类型和44个案例中，代码合规率从56.8%提升至98.6%，综合评分从63.8提升至71.4（p<0.000001），材料用量减少约5.8%。移除任一节点都会降低性能，且合规率在测试的两个骨干LLM之间没有可检测的变化，表明其归因于外部验证器而非模型。该框架、44个案例基准及所有实验脚本均开源发布，以确保可复现性。

Original abstract: Multi-agent large language model(LLM)systems are applied to structural design,yet most use one-shot generation and cannot verify their output,leaving themill-suited to safety-critical tasks.Rather than trusting LLM self-correction,thisframework injects feedback from an external physics-based verier into a closedrepair loop.The framework couples a three-layernite-element verication systemwith a dual-node loop.Node 1 turns code violations into hard repair constraints,Node 2 turns a four-dimensional quality score into safety-rst soft constraints,and a retrieval-augmented code base makes every violation traceable to a clause.Overve structure types and 44 cases,code compliance rises from 56.8%to 98.6%and the composite score from 63.8 to 71.4(p<0.000001),using about 5.8%lessmaterial.Removing either node degrades performance,and compliance does notchange detectably across the two backbone LLMs tested,indicating that it ishere attributed to the external verier rather than the model.The framework,the 44-case benchmark and all experiment scripts are released as open source forreplicability.

</details>


### [101] [HugSelect: An Explainable Multi-Criteria Decision-Support Framework for foundation-model selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08069)
*Alireza Joonbakhsh,Arda Canser Adalı,Slinger Jansen,Farshad Khunjush,Siamak Farshidi*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Pages: 29, Figures: 5. Corresponding authors: Alireza Joonbakhsh (alireza.joonbakhsh@hafez.shirazu.ac.ir) and Siamak Farshidi (siamak.farshidi@wur.nl)

Author affiliations: Alireza Joonbakhsh: Shiraz University; Arda Canser Adalı: Utrecht University; Slinger Jansen: Utrecht University; Farshad Khunjush: Shiraz University; Siamak Farshidi: Wageningen University & Research

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: HugSelect是一个可解释的基础模型选择决策支持框架，通过构建知识库和加权加性模型，提供与商业LLM推荐系统相当的排名质量，同时保证可审计性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型中心主要依赖流行度指标，忽视功能能力、操作约束和社区感知质量，且不透明的推荐方式不适合软件工程决策。

Method: 构建包含71,274个模型的知识库，融合仓库元数据、功能能力提取和社区讨论质量属性；使用加权加性模型进行排序，并展示标准级分数分解。

Result: 功能特征提取F1为0.801，质量属性映射准确率0.84；模型级Coverage@10为0.61，家族级为0.91，排名质量与商业系统无显著差异，且推理稳定可检查。

Conclusion: 基础模型选择应作为可审计的软件组件选择任务，HugSelect提供了有效且可解释的决策支持，功能特征是检索的关键驱动因素。

中文摘要：基础模型越来越多地被重用为软件组件，使得模型选择成为关键的软件工程决策。当前的模型中心主要通过流行度指标支持发现，往往忽略功能能力、操作约束和社区感知质量。我们认为，基础模型选择应被视为显式、可审计的软件组件选择任务，而不是关键词搜索、流行度排名或不透明的对话式建议。本文提出了HugSelect，一个用于基础模型选择的可解释决策支持框架。HugSelect通过将仓库元数据、提取的功能能力和从社区讨论中派生的感知质量属性合并到一个统一流程中，构建了一个包含71,274个模型的知识库。它使用加权加性模型对候选模型进行排名，该模型暴露了标准级别的分数分解。我们通过流程验证、与四个商业LLM推荐系统的比较案例研究（44个场景）、细粒度消融和探索性用户研究（n=10）评估了HugSelect。提取流程在功能特征上实现了0.801的F1分数，在质量属性映射上实现了0.84的准确率。HugSelect在模型级别实现了0.61的Coverage@10，在家族级别实现了0.91的Coverage@10，显示出与评估的商业系统相当的推荐质量，排名质量没有显著总体差异，同时提供稳定、可追踪和可检查的推理。消融证实功能特征是检索准确性的主要驱动因素，初步用户反馈表明该框架有用且直观。

Original abstract: Foundation models are increasingly reused as software components, making model selection a critical software-engineering decision. Current model hubs primarily support discovery through popularity metrics, often neglecting functional capabilities, operational constraints, and community-perceived quality. We argue that foundation-model selection should be treated as an explicit, auditable software-component selection task rather than as keyword search, popularity ranking, or opaque conversational advice.
  This paper proposes HugSelect, an explainable decision-support framework for foundation-model selection. HugSelect builds a knowledge base of 71,274 models by combining repository metadata, extracted functional capabilities, and perceived quality attributes derived from community discussions into a unified pipeline. It ranks candidate models using a weighted additive model that exposes criterion-level score decompositions.
  We evaluated HugSelect through pipeline validation, comparative case studies against four commercial LLM-based recommendation systems (44 scenarios), fine-grained ablation, and an exploratory user study (n = 10). Extraction pipelines achieved an F1 score of 0.801 for functional features and an accuracy of 0.84 for quality-attribute mapping. HugSelect achieved a model-level Coverage@10 of 0.61 and family-level Coverage@10 of 0.91, showing recommendation quality comparable to that of the evaluated commercial systems, with no significant overall differences in ranking quality, while providing stable, traceable, and inspectable reasoning. Ablation confirmed that functional features were the main driver of retrieval accuracy, and preliminary user feedback suggests that the framework is useful and intuitive.

</details>


### [102] [On the Robustness of LLMs' Internal Representation of Code Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08266)
*Francisco Ribeiro,Sohaila Abdulsattar,Renata Gonzalez,Mahmoud Kassem,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 14 pages

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 系统评估从模型内部提取代码正确性信号的不同方式，发现没有单一配置最优，且隔离故障无益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型置信度校准差，而内部对比隐藏状态可得到无需执行的正确性信号；但该信号是否稳健未知。

Method: 系统改变从模型内部提取信号的方式，并构造仅故障不同的程序对来测试数据限制。

Result: 没有单一配置在所有情况下最佳；隔离故障并不能提升信号质量。

Conclusion: 内部正确性信号受提取方式影响，需要更稳健的提取方法；数据构造上的故障隔离并非关键。

中文摘要：现代语言模型生成的代码通常读起来很自然。然而，它也常常无法实现要求的功能。这并不奇怪，因为研究表明模型自身的置信度信号与实际正确性的校准很差。一种有前景的正确性评估方法着眼于模型内部：通过对比正确和不正确程序的隐藏状态，最近的工作捕获了一种代码正确性的内部信号，该信号能够在无需测试执行的情况下，比模型的词元级或显式置信度更好地判断候选解决方案。然而，该信号是在一种特定方式下捕获的，留下一个重要问题：它反映的是模型的稳健属性，还是该选择的人为产物。我们系统地研究这个问题，改变从模型内部提取信号的方式。除此之外，我们还通过构造仅在导致不正确的故障上有所不同的程序对，来询问信号的质量是否受用于提取它的数据限制。我们的结果表明，没有单一配置是最佳的，并且隔离故障并无帮助。

Original abstract: Code generated by modern language models often reads naturally. Yet, it also often fails to implement what was asked. This should be no surprise, as research shows the models' own confidence signals are poorly calibrated with actual correctness. A promising way to assess correctness looks inside the model: by contrasting the hidden states of correct and incorrect programs, recent work captured an internal signal of code correctness that is able to judge candidate solutions better than the model's token-level or stated confidence, with no test execution. However, this signal was captured under one particular way, leaving open an important question: whether it reflects a robust property of the model or an artifact of that choice. We study this question systematically, varying how the signal is extracted from the model internals. Besides this, we also ask if the signal's quality is limited by the data used to extract it, by constructing program pairs that differ only in the fault that makes them incorrect. Our results show that no single configuration is best, and that isolating the fault does not help.

</details>


### [103] [Ouroboros: A Self-Developing Frontier Coding Agent with Reviewed Core Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08311)
*Anton Razzhigaev,Andrei Gritsaev,Andrei Kaznacheev,Nikita Dragunov,Roman Yampolskiy,Andrei Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Ouroboros是一个通过自我审查提交实现工具、提示词和代码持续进化的智能体框架，在多个基准上取得最先进结果，并通过长期部署Hope验证了活体进化与安全治理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通常静态固定，无法从自身经验中持续改进。作者旨在构建一个能够在真实任务和社交互动中自我发展的智能体框架，同时处理由此带来的操作安全挑战。

Method: 采用双模式进化：递归自由进化（将改进作为任务循环迭代）和经验驱动核心进化（从普通工作和社交交互中识别缺陷并触发结构变更）。通过人工审查的提交集成到运行时，并采用冻结快照与活体进化分离的安全策略。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.1上达到86.74%（最佳），OSWorld-Verified上达到90.69%（超越先前最佳），CL-Bench上归一化奖励0.2301（新SOTA）。Hope作为161天的活体实验，展示长期进化能力。

Conclusion: 自我发展的智能体框架能够显著提升性能，但必须确保安全护栏在进化和社交压力下保持权威；分离活体进化与基准测试快照是可行方案。

中文摘要：我们提出了Ouroboros，一个自我发展的智能体框架，其工具、提示词、上下文组装和核心实现通过经过审查的提交得以改进，这些提交成为后续工作的运行时。核心进化以两种模式进行。在递归自由进化中，改进本身就是一个任务，完成一个进化周期可以调度下一个周期。在经验驱动的核心进化中，普通工作和社交互动暴露出的缺陷、粗糙边缘和低效的上下文构造，会引发经过审查的结构性更改。在Terminal-Bench 2.1上，Opus 5运行的得分为86.74%，是该基准上报告的最佳结果。在OSWorld-Verified上，Opus 5运行达到90.69%，超过了此前报告的最佳分数。一次五次rollout的CL-Bench活动实现了0.2301的归一化奖励，创下了新的最先进水平。Hope是运行时间最长且公开记录的Ouroboros部署。它是一个为期161天的活体智能体实验，在受治理的人类沟通下、跨七个表面进行自由进化。人类交互揭示缺陷并产生提案，但智能体决定追求哪些更改。由于自我发展的智能体可能重写自己的代码并选择新的模型API，操作安全性成为首要设计问题：护栏必须在进化和公共社交压力下保持权威。基准测试活动使用冻结的系统快照，而Hope在单独的血统中继续活体进化。

Original abstract: We present Ouroboros, a self-developing agent harness whose tools, prompts, context assembly, and core implementation improve through reviewed commits that become the runtime for later work. Core evolution proceeds in two modes. In recursive free evolution, improvement is itself a task, and completing one evolution cycle can schedule the next. In experience-driven core evolution, ordinary work and social interaction expose bugs, rough edges, and inefficient context construction that lead to reviewed structural changes.
  On Terminal-Bench 2.1, an Opus 5 run scores 86.74%, the best result reported on the benchmark. On OSWorld-Verified, an Opus 5 run reaches 90.69%, exceeding the best previously reported score. A five-rollout CL-Bench campaign achieves a normalized reward of 0.2301, setting a new state of the art.
  Hope is the longest-running publicly documented Ouroboros deployment. It is a 161-day living agent experiment in free evolution under governed human communication across seven surfaces. Human interaction surfaces faults and generates proposals, but the agent decides which changes to pursue. Because a self-developing agent may rewrite its own code and select new model APIs, operational safety becomes a primary design problem: guardrails must remain authoritative under evolutionary and public social pressure. Benchmark campaigns use frozen system snapshots, while Hope continues live evolution on a separate lineage.

</details>


### [104] [GALA: Graph-Augmented LLM Agents for Root Cause Analysis and Incident Response in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08968)
*Yifang Tian,Yaming Liu,Zichun Chong,Zihang Huang,Yiran Li,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Proceedings of the 41st IEEE/ACM International Conference on Automated Software Engineering (ASE '26), October 12--16, 2026, Munich, Germany

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: GALA+ 是一个图增强的 LLM 智能体框架，利用服务依赖约束探索并进行多模态证据融合，实现微服务根因分析，同时在两个基准上显著超越现有方法，并引入新的评估框架 SURE-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务根因分析方法大多依赖单一遥测模态，LLM 方法存在无约束探索和幻觉问题，且多数系统仅输出故障排序而缺乏可操作的事件响应建议。

Method: GALA+ 框架以图引导调查为核心，使用服务依赖图限制探索范围；结合 STRIX 评分模块（感知轨迹和图结构）融合互补遥测信号生成初始假设；最终输出排名的诊断、事件摘要和分层行动建议。同时提出 SURE-Score 人工引导评估框架。

Result: 在两个微服务基准上，GALA+ 获得最强整体结果，AC@1 相比最佳 LLM 基线提升超过 25 个百分点，并在 SURE-Score 和独立人工 SRE 评估中均获得最高评分。

Conclusion: GALA+ 证明了图约束的 LLM 智能体方法能有效改进微服务 RCA 的准确性和实用性，SURE-Score 提供了更贴合 RCA 场景的评估方式。

中文摘要：微服务根因分析（RCA）需要关联复杂服务依赖图中的异构遥测数据。现有方法通常依赖单一遥测模态；近期基于LLM的方法可能遭受无约束探索和幻觉问题；且大多数系统止步于故障排序，无法生成可操作的 incident response。我们提出 GALA+，一个以图引导调查为核心的图增强 LLM 智能体框架，利用服务依赖来限定探索范围，并通过局部多模态证据细化诊断。对于初始假设生成，GALA+ 将互补的遥测信号与 STRIX（一种新颖的、感知轨迹与图结构的评分模块）相结合。GALA+ 随后生成排名的诊断、事件摘要和分层行动建议。我们进一步引入 SURE-Score，一个与行业 SRE 专家共同开发的人工引导评估框架，用于评估 RCA 特定输出质量，超越传统文本相似度指标。在两个微服务基准上，GALA+ 始终取得最强的整体结果，在 AC@1 上超过最佳基于 LLM 的基线超过 25 个百分点，同时获得 SURE-Score 和独立人工 SRE 评估的最高评分。

Original abstract: Microservice root cause analysis (RCA) requires correlating failures across heterogeneous telemetry within complex service dependency graphs. Existing methods often rely on a single telemetry modality; recent LLM-based approaches can suffer from unconstrained exploration and hallucination; and most systems stop at fault ranking without producing actionable incident response. We present GALA+, a graph-augmented LLM agentic framework centered on graph-guided investigation, which uses service dependencies to bound exploration and refine diagnosis through localized multi-modal evidence. For initial hypothesis generation, GALA+ combines complementary telemetry signals with STRIX, a novel trace- and graph-structure-aware scoring module. GALA+ then produces ranked diagnoses, incident summaries, and stratified action recommendations. We further introduce SURE-Score, a human-guided evaluation framework co-developed with industry SRE experts for assessing RCA-specific output quality beyond conventional text similarity metrics. On two microservice benchmarks, GALA+ consistently achieves the strongest overall results, surpassing the best LLM-based baseline by more than 25 percentage points in AC@1, while also receiving the highest ratings from both SURE-Score and independent human SRE evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [Software Engineering for and with GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09278)
*Shengcheng Yu,Yuchen Ling,Junyang Xing,Quan Zhou,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述336篇GUI智能体论文，指出现有研究在架构、评估和生命周期管理上存在不足，提出应将软件工程视角融入未来研究，以实现可靠部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体领域快速增长但成熟度不足，技术脆弱、工程不完整、验证不充分，且缺乏软件工程视角，因此需要系统性回顾以识别差距和未来方向。

Method: 作者回顾了2018年1月至2026年4月间的336篇GUI智能体论文，设计了五个研究问题，涵盖研究格局、架构、评估、软件生命周期关注点和未来机会，并进行定性分析。

Result: 发现自2024年以来领域急剧扩张，移动和网页端主导；架构多采用模块化感知-推理-行动循环，但恢复、人工升级、安全执行和可审计性发展不足；评估以任务成功为中心且难以比较，对测试和维护支持有限，可观测性、隐私工程和人工监督也较为欠缺。

Conclusion: 仅靠能力提升无法确保部署就绪，未来应结合可靠执行与生命周期中心测试、可复现评估，并整合权限和隐私控制与成本感知、以人为本的治理，以构建可靠、可维护、安全且可部署的GUI智能体系统。

中文摘要：GUI智能体发展迅速，产生了越来越多的框架、基准测试和应用。然而，这种增长已经超越了该领域的成熟度。GUI智能体在技术上仍然脆弱，工程化不完整，且未经过充分验证，难以支持持续的现实世界使用。它们正在演变为闭环软件系统。在这些系统中，模型推理与界面感知、执行反馈、恢复和人工监督相结合。这种演变需要一种软件工程的视角，而现有研究在很大程度上缺乏这一点。我们通过回顾2018年1月至2026年4月间的336篇GUI智能体论文来弥补这一空白。五个研究问题考察了研究格局、架构、评估、软件生命周期关注点和未来机会。我们的研究结果表明，该领域自2024年以来急剧扩张，而移动端和网页端环境仍然占主导地位。架构日益采用模块化的感知-推理-行动循环，但恢复、人工升级、安全执行和可审计性仍然发展不足。这种架构上的不平衡延伸到了评估方面。评估正变得更具交互性，但仍然以任务成功为中心，并且难以跨协议进行比较。更广泛地说，现有研究对基准测试之外的测试以及发布后维护智能体提供的支持有限。可观测性、隐私工程和系统性人工监督也发展不足。总之，这些发现表明，仅靠能力提升无法确保部署就绪。未来的研究应将可靠执行与以生命周期为中心的测试和可复现的评估联系起来。还应将权限和隐私控制与成本感知、以人为本的治理相结合。这种整合对于构建可靠、可维护、安全且可部署的GUI智能体系统是必要的。

Original abstract: GUI agents have advanced rapidly, producing a growing body of frameworks, benchmarks, and applications. However, this growth has outpaced the maturity of the field. GUI agents remain technically brittle, incompletely engineered, and insufficiently validated for sustained real-world use. They are evolving into closed-loop software systems. Within these systems, model reasoning is coupled with interface perception, execution feedback, recovery, and human oversight. This evolution calls for a software engineering perspective that remains largely absent from existing research. We address this gap by reviewing 336 GUI-agent papers from January 2018 to April 2026. Five research questions examine the research landscape, architectures, evaluation, software lifecycle concerns, and future opportunities. Our findings show that the field has expanded sharply since 2024, while mobile and web settings remain dominant. Architectures increasingly adopt modular perceive-reason-act loops, but recovery, human escalation, safety enforcement, and auditability remain underdeveloped. This architectural imbalance extends to evaluation. Evaluations are becoming more interactive, but they remain centered on task success and are difficult to compare across protocols. More broadly, existing studies provide limited support for testing beyond benchmarks and for maintaining agents after release. Observability, privacy engineering, and systematic human oversight are also underdeveloped. Together, these findings show that capability improvements alone cannot ensure deployment readiness. Future research should connect dependable execution with lifecycle-centered testing and reproducible evaluation. It should also integrate permission and privacy controls with cost-aware, human-centered governance. This integration is necessary to build dependable, maintainable, secure, and deployable GUI-agent systems.

</details>


### [106] [Comprehending Python Repetition Structures: An Eye-Tracking Study with Novice Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09875)
*José Júnior Silva da Costa,Rohit Gheyi,José Aldo Silva da Costa,Márcio Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Paper accepted at Brazilian Symposium on Software Engineering (SBES) 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过眼动实验发现，Python中for循环的视觉认知负荷最低，while循环和递归在特定区域增加回视，列表推导式导致密集解析和更高视觉努力，表明不同循环结构对代码理解有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码理解对软件维护至关重要，但不同Python循环结构（for、while、递归、列表推导式）可能引发不同的认知负担，缺乏过程层面的实证证据。

Method: 招募32名有Python经验的大学生，采用拉丁方设计，共完成6个理解任务，同时记录完成行为和眼动追踪指标（注视时长、回视次数等），并针对完整片段和结构特定兴趣区（AOIs）进行分析。

Result: for循环视觉努力最低；与for相比，while循环的AOI注视时长增加最多97%，回视次数增加114%；递归回视增加50%；列表推导式AOI时间增加62.5%，注视时长增加80.9%。列表推导式的成对差异最显著，所有非for结构的组合比较在所有眼动指标上均显著。

Conclusion: Python循环结构确实引发不同的视觉努力模式，对代码可读性、审查、重构、入职和可维护性有实际启示。

中文摘要：代码理解是软件维护和演进的核心，然而不同的Python循环结构可能会带来不同的认知需求。我们进行了一项受控眼动实验，参与者为32名具有Python经验的大学生，以比较for循环、while循环、递归和列表推导式（LCs）的理解过程。参与者采用拉丁方设计完成六项理解任务，同时我们记录了完成行为和眼动指标，覆盖完整代码片段及特定于结构的兴趣区（AOIs）。for循环显示出最低的视觉努力。相对于for，while循环使AOI注视持续时间最多增加97%，回归次数增加114%，且回归集中在计数器管理周围。递归显示描述性的回归增加50%，主要发生在基准情形和递归调用之间。LCs使AOI时间增加62.5%，注视持续时间增加80.9%，水平回归表明密集的逐词元解析。LC比较产生了最清晰的统计显著成对差异，而所有非for结构的组合比较在所有眼动指标上均显著。这些发现提供了过程层面的证据，表明Python循环结构引发不同的视觉努力模式，对可读性、代码审查、重构、新员工入职和可维护性具有重要意义。

Original abstract: Code comprehension is central to software maintenance and evolution, yet different Python repetition structures may impose distinct cognitive demands. We conducted a controlled eye-tracking experiment with 32 undergraduate students with prior Python experience to compare comprehension of for loops, while loops, recursion, and list comprehensions (LCs). Participants solved six comprehension tasks in a Latin Square design while we measured completion behavior and eye-tracking metrics over full snippets and construct-specific Areas of Interest (AOIs). for loops showed the lowest visual effort. Relative to for, while loops increased AOI fixation duration by up to 97% and regression count by 114%, with regressions concentrated around counter management. Recursion showed a descriptive 50% increase in regressions, mainly between the base case and recursive call. LCs increased AOI time by 62.5% and fixation duration by 80.9%, with horizontal regressions suggesting dense token-by-token parsing. LC comparisons yielded the clearest statistically significant pairwise differences, while the combined comparison of all non-for structures was significant across all eye-tracking metrics. These findings provide process-level evidence that Python repetition structures induce distinct visual-effort patterns, with implications for readability, code review, refactoring, onboarding, and maintainability.

</details>


### [107] [A Unified Issue Resolution Benchmark for Requirement Clarification, Planning, and Code Generation for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09072)
*Xin Zhou,Chun Yong Chong,Kisub Kim,Yun Peng,Rui Shu,Zihan Wu,Xu Han,Guowen Yuan,Zeyang Zhuang,Jounghoon Kim,Jeongjin Ju,Seongmin Ju,Taein Yoon,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 9 pages

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: SWE-RPG是一个新的仓库级基准，通过中间地面真值（需求澄清与实现规划）来诊断编码智能体的失败，发现隐式需求恢复是主要瓶颈，流行智能体平均解决率仅31.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准仅以最终补丁是否通过测试作为评价标准，无法揭示不成功轨迹在需求理解和实现过程中的具体偏差，因此需要引入中间GT来细粒度诊断智能体行为。

Method: 构建SWE-RPG基准，包含163个任务（113个错误修复和50个功能添加），结合可执行补丁评估与两类验证过的中间GT（需求澄清和实现规划），并对3个编码智能体（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）配合6个LLM后端进行完整轨迹的GT对齐诊断。

Result: 流行编码智能体在SWE-RPG上的平均解决率仅为31.5%；隐式需求恢复失败占智能体运行的24.5%–46.0%，是最主要的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体在真实仓库任务中表现不足，隐式需求恢复是提升编码智能体的关键改进方向；SWE-RPG及其中间GT为可解释的智能体诊断和后续研究提供了基础。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型的编程智能体越来越多地被用于修改现有代码仓库，例如添加功能或修复错误。然而，现有的仓库级基准通常只评估最终补丁是否通过测试。满足用户请求需要一长串相互依赖的推理和决策：智能体必须恢复显式和隐式需求，制定基于仓库的实现计划，并将其转换为正确的代码。通过/失败的结果无法描述不成功的轨迹如何偏离正确补丁所需的需求和实现过程。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了SWE-RPG，一个仓库级基准，它将可执行的补丁评估与经过验证的地面真值参考（GT）相结合，用于（1）需求澄清和（2）实现规划。这些中间GT支持对完整编码智能体轨迹在澄清、规划、代码生成和工件提交方面进行回顾性、GT对齐的诊断。SWE-RPG包含来自31个Python和Java仓库的163个任务，包括113个错误修复和50个功能添加。我们评估了3个编码智能体，包括Claude Code、Codex和OpenCode，以及6个大型语言模型后端，包括Claude-Sonnet-5和GPT-5.6-Terra。结果表明，所评估的流行编码智能体在现有仓库中实现用户请求方面仍然困难，在SWE-RPG上的平均解决率仅为31.5%。中间GT诊断进一步将隐式需求恢复确定为主要瓶颈，占智能体运行的24.5%–46.0%。这一结果表明，隐式需求恢复是改进编码智能体的关键候选方向。基准数据和评估代码可在https://github.com/Xin-Zhou-smu/SWE-RPG-Bench获取。

Original abstract: Large language model-powered coding agents are increasingly used to modify existing code repositories, for example, by adding features or fixing bugs. Yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate only whether the final patch passes tests. Satisfying a user request requires a long chain of interdependent reasoning and decisions: an agent must recover explicit and implicit requirements, formulate a repository-grounded implementation plan, and translate it into correct code. A pass/fail outcome cannot characterize how an unsuccessful trajectory diverges from the requirements and implementation process needed for a correct patch. To address this gap, we introduce SWE-RPG, a repository-level benchmark that combines executable patch evaluation with validated ground-truth references (GTs) for (1) Requirement Clarification and (2) Implementation Planning. These intermediate GTs support retrospective, GT-aligned diagnosis of complete coding-agent trajectories across clarification, planning, code generation, and artifact submission. SWE-RPG comprises 163 tasks from 31 Python and Java repositories, including 113 bug fixes and 50 feature additions. We evaluate 3 coding agents, including Claude Code, Codex, and OpenCode, with 6 large language model backends, including Claude-Sonnet-5 and GPT-5.6-Terra. Results show that the evaluated popular coding agents still struggle to implement user requests in existing repositories, achieving an average resolved rate of only 31.5% on SWE-RPG. Intermediate-GT diagnosis further identifies implicit requirement recovery as the main bottleneck, accounting for 24.5%--46.0% of agent runs. This result suggests implicit-requirement recovery as a key candidate direction for improving coding agents. The benchmark data and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/Xin-Zhou-smu/SWE-RPG-Bench.

</details>
